ROUND 200: ANDROID GAUGE-CARD FRAGMENT -- REFRAMED, ROUNDS 197-199 WERE THE WRONG DIAGNOSIS (2026-07-10)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx

- Round 199's fix (also 197, 198) had zero visible effect on the
  reported symptom, again. Marco ran his own experiment: swiping the
  WeekStrip to a different week makes the stray gauge-card fragment
  above it disappear; swiping back to the original week brings it
  back. This means the symptom is tied to TodayScreen's day-content
  carousel own render (the 3-panel prev/current/next day carousel,
  keyed by date), not a generic safe-area/top-padding issue --
  rounds 197-199 were solving the wrong problem the whole time, even
  though the underlying --safe-top/--safe-bottom centralization from
  round 199 is still correct and worth keeping for its own sake.
- HYPOTHESIS (not confirmed via direct DOM inspection -- no remote
  debugger access this session): the carousel's outer container uses
  paddingTop:24px + marginTop:-24px (gives the active panel's card
  shadow room without overflow:hidden clipping it, cancelling the
  padding's layout impact). This exact padding+negative-margin-cancel
  trick, combined with overflow:hidden and a transformed sliding
  child, is a known category of cross-WebView-engine rendering
  inconsistency, and lines up with the remount-clears-it /
  remount-reproduces-it behavior Marco observed (panels are keyed by
  date, so switching weeks fully unmounts/remounts them).
- FIX (experimental): dropped the negative-margin cancellation on
  Android only (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android') -- keeps the
  paddingTop for shadow room, costs at most 24px of harmless extra
  spacing on Android if this hypothesis is wrong. iOS untouched.
- NOT YET CONFIRMED. Awaiting on-device retest.
- tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 57 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).

ROUND 199: ANDROID STATUS-BAR FIX, THE ACTUAL FIX (CAPACITOR'S REAL INJECTED VALUE) (2026-07-10)
================================================================
Files: src/index.css, src/main.tsx, src/ui/AppShell.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/GoalSetupScreen.tsx, src/ui/kit/SlideScreen.tsx,
       src/ui/components/Toaster.tsx, src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx,
       src/ui/kit/Sheet.tsx, src/ui/kit/FloatingTabBar.tsx

- ROUNDS 197-198 BOTH GUESSED WRONG: round 198's on-device result was
  identical, pixel for pixel, to round 197's -- a strong signal the
  fix wasn't reaching the device, or was solving the wrong problem.
  Marco asked for a careful-mode pass: read whatever "special tweak"
  iOS relies on and actually understand the mechanism before touching
  code again.
- REAL MECHANISM FOUND (by reading Capacitor's own source, not
  guessing): node_modules/@capacitor/android/.../plugin/SystemBars.java
  measures the real native inset via WindowInsetsCompat and injects it
  into the WebView as a CSS *custom property*,
  --safe-area-inset-top/right/bottom/left (var() syntax) -- a
  different mechanism from the CSS *environment variable*
  env(safe-area-inset-top), which Android's WebView always resolves to
  a real, defined 0px (confirmed: rounds 197-198's env()/fallback
  tricks could never have worked, regardless of exact syntax, because
  the fallback branch was never reachable). Our code only ever read
  env(), so Android's real, precise, already-injected value was sitting
  unused the whole time. Confirmed iOS is unaffected by checking its
  equivalent native plugin (SystemBars.swift): it never sets that
  custom property, so iOS falls straight through to its real, native
  env() value exactly as before -- zero behavior change there.
- FIX: index.css now defines root --safe-top/--safe-bottom vars that
  read Capacitor's real injected custom property first, falling back
  to env() (iOS/desktop's existing path) only if unset:
  var(--safe-area-inset-top, env(safe-area-inset-top, 0px)). Every
  safe-area usage across the app was migrated to read these two vars
  instead of duplicating env() inline: AppShell's status-bar fade +
  tap-interceptor, GoalSetupScreen's sticky-header spacer, SlideScreen,
  Toaster, AddEntrySheet (x3), Sheet.tsx (x3), FloatingTabBar. Removed
  round 197/198's --safe-top-override 32px guessed constant --
  superseded, no longer needed.
- NOT YET DONE: needs actual on-device confirmation before considering
  this closed, given two prior fixes both silently failed. See
  PROJECT-CONTEXT.txt's KNOWN GAPS.
- tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 57 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).

================================================================
ROUND 198: ANDROID STATUS-BAR FIX, TAKE TWO -- env() FALLBACK NEVER TRIGGERS ON ANDROID (2026-07-10)
================================================================
Files: src/index.css, src/main.tsx

- Round 197's fix had literally zero visible effect on-device, even
  after confirming the web bundle was actually rebuilt (npx cap sync
  android alone does NOT rebuild -- npm run android:sync / npm run
  build does; this was also independently confirmed as a real gotcha
  during this round's debugging).
- ROOT CAUSE OF THE NON-FIX: CSS env(name, fallback)'s fallback value
  only applies when the engine doesn't support/recognize the named
  environment variable at all. Android's Chromium-based WebView DOES
  recognize env(safe-area-inset-top) -- it just always resolves it to
  a real, defined 0px, since Android doesn't wire native WindowInsets
  into the WebView's environment variables the way iOS's WKWebView
  does. Nesting var(--safe-top-fallback, 0px) inside env()'s own
  fallback slot (round 197) therefore never actually ran, because
  env()'s "undefined" condition was never met on Android.
- FIX: flipped the check order. .safe-top now reads
  var(--safe-top-override, env(safe-area-inset-top, 0px)) --
  var()'s own fallback correctly triggers whenever the custom property
  isn't set, which is true everywhere except native Android (main.tsx
  only calls setProperty('--safe-top-override', ...) there), so the
  override reliably wins on Android while iOS (real inset) and
  desktop/browser preview (unset, 0px, unchanged) are unaffected.
- tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 57 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).

================================================================
ROUND 197: ANDROID SAFE-AREA-INSET-TOP FIX (STATUS-BAR OVERLAP, REAL ROOT CAUSE) (2026-07-10)
================================================================
Files: src/index.css, src/main.tsx

- ROOT CAUSE FOUND: on-device (Pixel 9 Pro emulator) the Diary/Today
  screen's top content (WeekStrip / gauge card) rendered under the
  status bar/clock — round 196's targetSdkVersion 36->34 change did
  NOT fix it, disproving that the sole cause was Android 15's
  edge-to-edge enforcement. Actual cause: Android's WebView does not
  populate env(safe-area-inset-top) the way iOS's WKWebView does. The
  shared .safe-top utility class (index.css) used a flat 0px fallback,
  so every screen using it (AppShell's <main>, GoalSetupScreen,
  OnboardingFlow, FirstOpenFork, OnboardingDailyAllowance, GoalScreen,
  TopBar, SlideScreen) silently got zero top padding on Android.
- FIX: .safe-top's fallback is now
  env(safe-area-inset-top, var(--safe-top-fallback, 0px)). main.tsx
  sets --safe-top-fallback to an estimated 32px on native Android only
  (Capacitor.getPlatform() === 'android'), leaving iOS (real inset,
  always non-zero there) and desktop/browser preview (unset, stays
  0px, no visual change) untouched.
- NOT YET DONE: 32px is an estimate, not measured per-device. See
  PROJECT-CONTEXT.txt's KNOWN GAPS. .safe-bottom/.safe-pb use the same
  pattern and weren't audited this round — not reported broken on the
  emulator, but unconfirmed.
- tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 57 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).

  content clipped under the status bar on first device run.

  apps targeting SDK 35+ (our default targetSdkVersion was 36).
  Lowered targetSdkVersion 36->34 to opt out — no Play Store policy

  did NOT fully fix the issue — see round 197 for the actual root

ROUND 193: DIARY SIGN-DISPLAY FIX; WEEKLY-STRIP PER-DAY BMR FIX; GAIN PACE RENAME; WHEELPICKER HAPTICS; SHARED-BETA MODE FOR EXTERNAL TESTERS (2026-07-09)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx, src/ui/kit/WheelPicker.tsx,
       src/domain/goal.ts, src/ui/screens/GoalSetupScreen.tsx,
       src/lib/sharedBeta.ts (new), src/data/db.ts, src/lib/apiKey.ts,
       src/ui/components/DevMenu.tsx, src/ui/screens/AccountScreen.tsx,
       .env.example, TESTFLIGHT.md

- DAY'S LOG SIGN DISPLAY FIXED: the gauge card's Food/Activity stat row
  was already correct and goal-type-agnostic (Food always plain, Activity
  always "−"), but the Day's log list below it branched on gainGoal and
  had both signs backwards for lose_by_date/maintain (Food showed "−",
  Activity showed "+"), only accidentally correct for gain_by_date.
  Removed the branching entirely so the list always matches the gauge
  card, across all three goal types.
- WEEKLY STRIP PER-DAY BMR FIX: the week strip's succeed/fail dots used a
  single flat user.bmr for all 21 days, while the day panel/gauge card
  (via useDay -> bmrForDate) computes BMR per day from whichever weight
  was on file as of that specific date. Any day whose historical weight
  differed from the current one could disagree between the two (Marco
  hit this testing with a 5-day-old start date and mid-test weight
  changes: gauge card said on-target, strip showed a fail X). Strip now
  calls bmrForDate(d, weights, user) per day, matching the day panel
  exactly, with the same estimate-from-weight fallback for incomplete
  profiles.
- GAIN PACE TIERS RENAMED: Guided goal-setup's Gain "Pace" tiers were
  Lean/Steady/Bulk (GAIN_PACES in domain/goal.ts) -- body-composition
  outcome language, inconsistent with Lose's Relaxed/Steady/Ambitious
  (effort/speed language), even though both are mechanically the same
  thing: three tiers ordered slow-to-fast driving an end date + daily
  kcal. Renamed to Relaxed/Steady/Ambitious to match Lose. IDs/values
  unchanged (kgPerMonth, surplusFloor/Ceiling); nothing persisted
  references the old ids (gainPace is transient setup-screen state
  only), so no migration needed. GoalSetupScreen's default state
  updated 'lean' -> 'relaxed' to match.
- WHEELPICKER HAPTICS: all 5 real user-driven onChange sites across
  WheelPicker's single-wheel and split-wheel (empty-state and normal)
  modes now call hapticLight(), matching the app-wide rule that
  interactive elements fire haptics on tap. The two mount-time
  auto-sync useEffect calls (snapping an out-of-range value on load)
  deliberately do NOT fire haptics, since nothing was tapped.
- SHARED-BETA MODE (new, for handing this app to external testers
  without requiring their own Anthropic key): a single build-time
  switch, VITE_SHARED_BETA (+ VITE_SHARED_BETA_ANTHROPIC_KEY) in
  .env.local (gitignored, never committed -- see .env.example), OFF
  unless explicitly set so Marco's own day-to-day build is never
  affected. New src/lib/sharedBeta.ts is the single source of truth for
  both flags. When on: a fresh install with no explicit profile choice
  yet defaults into the Test account instead of Real (src/data/db.ts's
  activeProfile -- an explicit stored choice via the Developer menu's
  profile switcher always wins over this default either way); AI Food
  Scan auto-connects using the shared key while on the Test profile
  only, never persisted to the keychain (src/lib/apiKey.ts's
  withSharedBetaFallback); the manual "AI Food Scan" Settings row is
  hidden entirely (AccountScreen.tsx), since there's nothing for a
  tester to configure. The hidden Developer menu (triple-tap Account
  title) shows a warning banner whenever a build has this mode baked
  in, as a safety net against forgetting to turn it off before a
  regular sync. TESTFLIGHT.md documents the full on/archive/off
  workflow, including that ending the test for everyone (at any time,
  no app update needed) is done by deleting the temporary key in the
  Claude Console -- instant and permanent; there's no per-key spend
  limit, so a dedicated Workspace spend limit, or prepaid credits with
  auto-reload off, is the safety net while the key is still live.
- tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 57 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged --
  these are UI-display/derivation fixes and a new opt-in build flag,
  verified by tracing the existing logic and matching it against the
  on-device repro Marco described, not new automated tests; see
  KNOWN GAPS).

ROUND 192: TESTFLIGHT UPLOAD FIX; EXPORT-JSON iOS FIX; STALE APP NAME (2026-07-08)
================================================================
Files: src/data/exportJson.ts, src/ui/screens/AccountScreen.tsx,
       ios/App/App/Info.plist, ios/App/App.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj,
       HEALTHKIT.md, package.json (added @capacitor/filesystem,
       @capacitor/share)

- EXPORT ALL DATA (JSON) FIXED ON iOS: Account > Developer > "Export all
  data (JSON)" did nothing when tapped on device. Root cause: it used a
  browser-style <a download> click trigger, which is a no-op inside the
  app's iOS webview (no Downloads folder, no default handler for it
  there); it only ever worked in a desktop browser during web dev.
  Rewrote exportJson.ts to use @capacitor/filesystem (write the JSON to
  the app's cache dir) plus @capacitor/share (open the native iOS share
  sheet) on native platforms, so the export now surfaces Save to Files,
  AirDrop, etc. The plain browser download path is kept as a fallback
  for web dev. AccountScreen's onClick now catches/logs a rejected
  promise instead of letting it fail silently. Confirmed working on
  device.
- TESTFLIGHT UPLOAD FAILURE FIXED: first upload attempt was rejected by
  Apple's automated Info.plist scan with "Missing purpose string,
  NSHealthUpdateUsageDescription". leve's own code only ever requests
  read access to Health (weight, calories), never write, but the
  @capgo/capacitor-health plugin links HealthKit's write-capable APIs
  regardless, and Apple's static binary scan requires a purpose string
  for any linked API, not just the ones actually called. Added
  NSHealthUpdateUsageDescription to Info.plist stating leve does not
  write to Health. HEALTHKIT.md updated to note both usage strings are
  required and why. Hit one snag getting this onto the device: Xcode's
  Info tab had reordered (not changed) the existing keys in the local
  Info.plist, which blocked `git pull` until the local copy was
  discarded via `git checkout --`, since git won't silently overwrite
  uncommitted local changes even when they are purely cosmetic. Second
  upload attempt cleared the check and is now Processing in App Store
  Connect.
- STALE APP DISPLAY NAME FIXED: the Xcode target's
  INFOPLIST_KEY_CFBundleDisplayName build setting (in
  project.pbxproj, both Debug and Release configs) still read "nutri"
  from before the app was renamed to leve, overriding Info.plist's own
  already-correct CFBundleDisplayName. Updated both to "leve".
- App Category (Healthcare & Fitness) was set directly in App Store
  Connect's web dashboard, unrelated to any file in this repo, no code
  change needed.
- tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 57 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).

ROUND 191: TESTFLIGHT DISTRIBUTION; APPLE HEALTH INTEGRATION; BRING-YOUR-OWN CLAUDE KEY + AI CONNECTION GATE; ACCOUNT SUB-VIEWS (2026-07-08)
================================================================
Files: capacitor.config.ts, ios/App/App/Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/
       AppIcon-512@2x.png, ios/App/App/Info.plist, TESTFLIGHT.md (new),
       HEALTHKIT.md (new), src/data/healthkit.ts (new), src/domain/types.ts,
       src/domain/calc.ts, src/lib/apiKey.ts (new), src/lib/foodScan.ts,
       src/lib/scanErrors.ts (new), src/ui/AppShell.tsx,
       src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx, src/ui/components/ApiKeySheet.tsx
       (new), src/ui/components/HealthConnectBanner.tsx (new),
       src/ui/components/PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryNewFoodScan.tsx, src/ui/components/Toaster.tsx,
       src/ui/components/WeightLogSheet.tsx, src/ui/components/useAiGate.tsx
       (new), src/ui/components/useFoodCapture.tsx, src/ui/kit/Icon.tsx,
       src/ui/kit/SlideScreen.tsx (new), src/ui/kit/index.ts,
       src/ui/screens/AccountScreen.tsx, src/ui/screens/GoalScreen.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/PastGoalsScreen.tsx, src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx,
       package.json (added @capgo/capacitor-health,
       @aparajita/capacitor-secure-storage, @capacitor/app)

- BUG FIXES CARRIED OVER FROM ROUND 190: the Diary badge stuck on "No
  goal set" for the newly-shipped maintain_weight goal type (badge logic
  only checked lose/gain fields), and Past Goals' "Total days" showing a
  bogus 3650 for maintain goals (no targetDate to diff against, fell
  back to a 10-year sentinel). Both fixed in TodayScreen.tsx/
  PastGoalsScreen.tsx by branching on isMaintainGoal() the same way the
  rest of the app already does.
- ACCOUNT: SegmentedControl REPLACED WITH FilterPills (sex, weight units,
  weigh-in frequency in ProfileSheet/WeightUnitsCard/WeightCadenceCard),
  matches the pattern Goal Setup already used, for visual consistency
  across the app's two-and-three-option toggles.
- TESTFLIGHT DISTRIBUTION SET UP: fixed two blockers (app icon had an
  alpha channel, App Store Connect requires none, flattened to RGB;
  capacitor.config.ts's appId didn't match the Xcode project's real
  bundle ID, aligned to com.marcosilva86.nutritiongoal) and wrote
  TESTFLIGHT.md, a full step-by-step runbook (team/cert setup, App Store
  Connect app record, archive/upload, Export Compliance, Internal
  Testing). Confirmed Internal Testing needs no Beta App Review and the
  existing `npm run ios` / Xcode ▶ Run cable workflow is untouched and
  stays exactly as fast, TestFlight is a separate, occasional channel,
  not a replacement.
- APPLE HEALTH (HEALTHKIT) INTEGRATION SHIPPED, real, on-device, no
  backend, via @capgo/capacitor-health. Scope: weight (read into the same
  weight history as manual entries, tagged source:'healthkit', never
  overwrites a date already logged by hand) and activity/active-energy
  (additive: syncs at most one Activity entry per day, tagged
  source:'healthkit', refreshed in place as more of the day's data
  lands, coexisting with any manual entries that day rather than
  replacing them). Design went through several rounds of on-device
  feedback and converged on: (1) sync runs quietly on app open/foreground
  only, no continuous polling, plus on-demand from a synced day's own
  row; (2) no historical backfill, sync is clamped to never reach
  earlier than the moment Connect was actually tapped (connectedAt),
  so connecting after already using the app manually for days doesn't
  silently backfill weeks of data on top of existing entries; (3) a
  synced Activity row opens a read-only view (SyncedActivitySheet, not
  the normal edit form) with exactly two actions, an eye icon to
  Hide/un-hide the entry from every calorie total while it stays visible
  in Day's log with muted styling (ActivityEntry.hidden, a toggle, not a
  delete, a hidden day is also skipped by future syncs so it doesn't
  silently reappear), and a refresh icon to sync just that day on demand.
  Root-caused and fixed an early undercount bug (254kcal shown vs. 514
  actual): summing raw readSamples() client-side misses HealthKit's own
  overlapping-source deduplication (iPhone + Watch + a workout app each
  writing their own samples) and risked truncating before reaching
  today's most recent data at the sample-count limit, switched to
  Health.queryAggregated({bucket:'day', aggregation:'sum'}), which
  matches the Health app's own numbers. Account gained a connect/
  disconnect entry (now inside the Settings sub-view, see below) with a
  live status dot + description; contextual "Connect Apple Health"
  banners were added to Log Activity and Log Weight (HealthConnectBanner,
  dismissible nudge when not connected, persistent non-dismissible
  status line with relative last-sync time once connected, so connecting
  has a visible ongoing result instead of a nudge just vanishing). The
  one manual step: HealthKit's capability + entitlement has to be added
  via Xcode's Signing & Capabilities UI (Apple provisions this against
  the App ID, can't be done by hand-editing project files reliably);
  documented in HEALTHKIT.md along with the exact Xcode steps.
- BRING-YOUR-OWN CLAUDE API KEY (BYOK) FOR AI FOOD SCAN: Account >
  Settings > "AI Food Scan" opens a full-height sheet (ApiKeySheet) where
  a user pastes their own Anthropic API key, stored via
  @aparajita/capacitor-secure-storage (iOS Keychain / Android Keystore,
  not localStorage), with a masked (password-style) field with an eye
  toggle to reveal it, and numbered in-app instructions for getting a key
  from console.anthropic.com. When a personal key is set, lib/foodScan.ts
  calls api.anthropic.com directly from the device (model claude-sonnet-5,
  forced tool_use for structured ScannedFood[] output via Anthropic's
  anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access header) instead of going
  through the shared Vercel proxy, confirmed working end-to-end on a
  real device with a live key. No key set falls back to the existing
  shared proxy, unchanged for everyone who hasn't opted in.
- AI-FEATURE CONNECTION GATE (useAiGate): per Marco's explicit ask,
  replaced reactive "try the scan, show an error if it fails" handling
  with an upfront check. Every AI-feature trigger (Camera, Photo,
  Describe, Nutri-scan, and "Fix"/re-describe on an already-scanned item,
  across the Day's-log basket's two internal copies, the shared Pantry
  capture hook, and all three Pantry surfaces) now checks for a cached
  personal key immediately on tap, before the camera/picker/describe
  overlay ever opens. No key cached shows a short sheet ("Connect AI to
  use this", View more, which jumps straight to the ApiKeySheet above,
  or Cancel, which just closes) instead of proceeding. lib/apiKey.ts's
  in-memory key cache is warmed once on app boot so this check is
  synchronous. Reactive error handling (FoodScanError, toastScanError)
  was kept as a second layer for failures the gate can't predict, an
  invalid/rejected key, a network blip, or the shared proxy itself being
  down, showing a clear message plus a tappable action into the same
  key sheet, at every scan/describe/label-scan call site. This also
  fixed a real bug Marco caught while testing: removing a saved key used
  to silently keep working via the shared-proxy fallback; now the very
  next AI action after removing a key hits the gate instead.
- ACCOUNT RESTRUCTURED: Tracking and Settings are no longer shown inline
  on the main Account view, they're reached via a small "Options"
  section (two-row menu, Tracking/Settings) below Profile, each opening
  its own full-screen sub-view that slides in from the right with a back
  chevron (same treatment as Past goals), and now also responds to a
  swipe-right gesture to go back. Developer stays nested inside the
  Settings sub-view, not its own menu row, since it's a hidden
  triple-tap-title reveal, not a normal entry point. SlideScreen/
  SlideHeader were extracted out of PastGoalsScreen.tsx into
  src/ui/kit/SlideScreen.tsx (a proper shared kit atom, not duplicated
  per screen) and gained two new opt-in props used only by Tracking/
  Settings: `onBack` (enables the swipe gesture, same 60px/mostly-
  horizontal threshold Sheet's own OverlayLayer swipe uses) and `muted`
  (matches Account's own grey bg-surface-muted page backdrop instead of
  Past goals' white-sheet look), both additive, so Past goals' existing
  behavior and appearance are unchanged.
- TEXT/COPY POLISH: ApiKeySheet's "How to get a key" instructions bumped
  to 16pt (bold title, regular body) for readability. Swept every line
  of prose added across this entire round (comments and user-facing
  copy) for em dashes per Marco's standing preference, replacing them
  with plain punctuation, scoped precisely via diff against the
  session's first commit so the codebase's own pre-existing writing

- tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 57 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged, no

ROUND 190 — MAINTAIN-WEIGHT GOAL TYPE; GOAL-SETUP UI POLISH; KEYBOARD DONE-BAR ROOT CAUSE; NEW-FOOD/DESCRIBE FLOW FIXES; GOAL-FORK NAVIGATION (2026-07-07)

Files: src/domain/types.ts, src/domain/goal.ts, src/domain/goal.test.ts,
       src/ui/screens/GoalSetupScreen.tsx, src/ui/screens/FirstOpenFork.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/GoalIcon.tsx (new, extracted from GoalSetupScreen.tsx),
       src/ui/screens/GoalScreen.tsx, src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/PastGoalsScreen.tsx, src/ui/screens/AccountScreen.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/PantryScreen.tsx, src/ui/kit/WheelPicker.tsx,
       src/ui/kit/useKeyboardDoneBar.tsx, src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryPicker.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryNewFoodScan.tsx,

- MAINTAIN-WEIGHT GOAL TYPE SHIPPED: the third goal type (alongside
  lose_by_date/gain_by_date) is now fully implemented end-to-end.
  domain/types.ts gained weightRangeFloorKg/weightRangeCeilingKg,
  maintainBandId, and reviewDate on Goal. domain/goal.ts added
  isMaintainGoal/usesKcalBand/inBand helpers and MAINTAIN_BANDS presets
  (Tight +/-0.5kg/+/-100kcal, Standard +/-1kg/+/-150kcal, Relaxed
  +/-2kg/+/-250kcal); requiredDailyDeficit returns 0 for maintain.
  GoalSetupScreen: Guided mode picks a band preset via FilterPills with a
  live-computed weight-range + calorie-band summary card; Detailed mode
  adds a tappable pill opening a modal to choose Tight/Standard/Relaxed/

  TodayScreen, PastGoalsScreen all branch three ways now (previously

  pre-existing AccountScreen bug in the same pass: the goal-summary line
  hardcoded "lose Xkg" for every goal type, including gain goals.
- GOAL-SETUP UI POLISH (multiple rounds of on-device feedback): the
  Detailed mode's weight-range picker was redesigned from a free-text
  min/max pair to the "picker playground picker 3" single-wheel style
  (WheelPicker gained a `mode?: 'auto' | 'single'` prop forcing one flat
  wheel even for decimal steps, plus a float-precision fix in its snap()
  helper -- 71.3 no longer became 71.30000000000001 and broke the
  picker's selected-value match). That same single-wheel mode was later
  extended to the Start/Current/Target weight fields for Lose-weight and
  Build-muscle too (previously only Maintain used it), per follow-up
  feedback. Guided mode's single Weight+Range card and Detailed mode's
  single Goal-name+Weight+Dates card were each split into individual
  per-section cards (container styling unchanged, just separated).
  AccountScreen's weigh-in weekday pills were unified onto the shared
  FilterPills component (previously hand-rolled, visibly smaller than
  GoalSetupScreen's own).
- GOAL-FORK NAVIGATION TRANSITIONS FIXED (device-confirmed working):
  going back from Goal-setup to the goal-type fork screen (both the
  first-open onboarding fork and the regular in-app fork reached from
  Today) had two related bugs. (1) The fork screen only mounted AFTER
  Goal-setup's slide-out-right exit animation fully finished (route
  change happens on a delayed setTimeout), so the animation revealed a
  blank background for ~300ms before the destination screen popped in.
  (2) Returning to the fork screen when it was originally reached via
  Today (?from=today) replayed its whole slide-up "new modal" entrance
  animation every time, since a browser-back (POP) navigation to that
  same URL still carries the query param. Fixed by having GoalSetupScreen
  render a static, non-interactive peek of the correct destination screen
  (new FirstOpenForkBackdrop / GoalForkBackdrop components in
  FirstOpenFork.tsx) underneath itself while its own exit animation
  plays, so "back" reveals it directly instead of a gap; the entrance
  animation is now gated on useNavigationType() !== 'POP' so it only
  plays on a genuine fresh visit. GoalIcon was extracted out of
  GoalSetupScreen.tsx into its own file to avoid a circular import
  between the two screens.
- KEYBOARD "DONE" BAR GAP -- ROOT CAUSE FOUND AND FIXED (see KNOWN GAPS
  for confirmation status): the long-running "Done bar floats a few px
  above the keyboard" report (open since round 181) was chased across
  several attempts this round. A first hypothesis -- Capacitor's
  keyboardWillShow height excludes env(safe-area-inset-bottom) -- was
  implemented and made things dramatically WORSE (Marco: "completely out
  of place"), so it was reverted. Real root cause was then found using
  on-device diagnostics Marco captured via two new rows added to Dev >
  Keyboard playground (a live readout of keyboardInset/innerHeight/
  visualViewport.height/safe-area-inset-bottom, plus a 5th row using the
  actual production useKeyboardDoneBar hook on a plain text field): the
  Done bar is `position: fixed`, which normally positions relative to the
  true viewport -- EXCEPT when a transformed ancestor sits between it and
  the root, which becomes its containing block instead (any non-'none'
  CSS transform triggers this, even an identity translateX(0)). Sheet's
  own OverlayLayer (used by "Edit food item" and every other nested
  overlay) always has an active transform for its slide-in animation, so
  a Done bar rendered inside an overlay was positioning against THAT
  panel, not the screen -- matching Sheet.tsx's own existing comments
  about forceExpanded (91dvh) panels drifting slightly from the true
  viewport once the keyboard is open in this WKWebView setup (see its
  pre-existing "Surface cover" div, a patch for a different symptom of
  the same dvh quirk). Confirmed via A/B: a Done bar on a plain top-level
  Sheet field was pixel-perfect; the same hook nested inside an overlay
  showed the gap. Fix: useKeyboardDoneBar's bar now renders via
  createPortal straight to document.body (the same escape-hatch Sheet
  itself already uses for ancestor-constraint problems), so it always
  positions against the real viewport regardless of nesting depth.
- "+ NEW FOOD" / DESCRIBE FLOW FIXES: the Day's-log basket's "Change"
  button (re-run Describe on a scanned item) was wired for the initial
  scan-review step (previously only worked once editing an
  already-logged entry). Pantry > + New food > Describe now slides in as
  a genuine side-panel overlay (Sheet's useSheetSetOverlay/OverlayLayer)
  instead of swapping content in place, and expands the Sheet to full
  90dvh height while open (per follow-up feedback that it felt cramped)
  with the textarea autofocused so the keyboard is already up. Root-
  caused and fixed a bug where this overlay didn't open at all on first
  landing: useSheetSetOverlay/useSheetSetOverlayBack were being called in
  the same component that renders <Sheet>, one level ABOVE the context
  Sheet provides to its own children, so the registration was silently a
  no-op -- moved into a new PantryNewFoodBody child component, matching
  every other useSheetSetOverlay call site in the app. AnalyzingIndicator
  gained a `compact` prop (smaller padding) used only here, replacing an
  earlier attempt that instead padded the shorter states UP to match a
  tall loading screen (reverted per feedback: "too much negative
  screen"). Manual food creation now also lands on the Food-item detail
  preview in "just created" mode (previously only scanned items did), so
  it gets the same "Save food" CTA and discard-on-close confirmation.
- SCROLLBAR CHROME HIDDEN APP-WIDE (device-confirmed): on-device, the
  visible WKWebView scrollbar track was poking through the native
  keyboard's own rounded top corners as a stray grey sliver while
  scrolling with the keyboard open. Added a global
  scrollbar-width:none / ::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none} rule
  (src/index.css) -- content scrolls identically, just without visible
  scrollbar chrome, matching how native iOS apps look.
- PANTRY SEED / MEALS MIGRATION NOW PER-PROFILE: both one-time seed flags
  (default pantry items, meals migration) were stored under a single
  global localStorage key shared across the app's real/test dual-profile
  system, so seeding the real account permanently skipped seeding the
  test account (and vice versa). Both flags are now scoped per active
  profile (defaultPantrySeedFlagKey/mealsMigrationFlagKey); DevMenu's
  test-account reset paths clear them too so wiping the test DB reseeds
  it. Added a regression test asserting the flag key differs per profile.
- tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 57 passed (6 new), 8 skipped.
  `npm run build` verified clean after every sub-batch this round.

ROUND 181 — KEYBOARD/UX POLISH BATCH: DONE BAR, CARB CAP, GOAL ICONS, INFO CARD, STATUS-BAR FADE, SHEET SHADOW FIXES, GOAL FORK ENTRY (2026-07-06)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/kit/useKeyboardDoneBar.tsx (renamed from useNumericDoneBar.tsx),
       src/ui/kit/LabeledInput.tsx, src/ui/kit/NumberField.tsx,
       src/ui/kit/Field.tsx, src/ui/screens/PantryScreen.tsx,
       src/domain/goal.ts, src/ui/kit/Icon.tsx, src/ui/screens/GoalScreen.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/GoalSetupScreen.tsx, src/ui/AppShell.tsx,
       src/ui/kit/Sheet.tsx, src/lib/useKeyboardInset.ts
- KEYBOARD DONE BAR UNIFIED: useNumericDoneBar renamed useKeyboardDoneBar
  and moved into LabeledInput so every text field (not just numeric ones)
  gets the same "Done" dismiss accessory bar as the numeric keyboard.
  NumberField simplified to just render LabeledInput. An early attempt
  forced the bar to a fixed 44px height on the assumption numeric/text
  variants rendered differently — Marco reported it "got even bigger";
  re-investigation confirmed both cases always shared the exact same
  component, so the forced height was reverted back to natural py-2
  sizing (DONE_BAR_HEIGHT corrected 44 -> ~40, see KNOWN GAPS re: this
  being a CSS-math estimate, not device-measured).
- BALANCED CARB TARGET CAP: activityCarbTargetG() (src/domain/goal.ts)
  now applies a 4.5g/kg hard cap for the Balanced macro style only
  (CARB_MODEL.balanced.capPerKg); Performance stays uncapped. Fixes the
  ~400g/day carb target outlier Marco saw on high-activity days under
  Balanced. Cap value itself is a first estimate, not yet validated.
- GOAL-OPTIONS AFFORDANCE: the "⋯" (moreHoriz) options icon in
  GoalScreen's two triggers was first swapped for a custom Marco-supplied
  gear icon (settingsGear, added to Icon.tsx), then replaced again per
  follow-up feedback with a plain "Options" text label
  (<Button variant="ghost" ...>Options</Button>). settingsGear and its
  Icon.tsx wiring were fully removed again afterward; moreHoriz itself
  is still defined but now unused anywhere in the app (see KNOWN GAPS).
- 44×44PT CLEAR-BUTTON HIT AREAS: audited and fixed all three clear (X)
  button implementations to meet the app's own touch-target rule.
  LabeledInput's absolutely-positioned button resized directly to
  h-11 w-11 (44×44) with the icon centered. Field's and PantryScreen's
  search-bar in-flow buttons use a -m-[14px]/p-[14px] padding +
  negative-margin trick that expands the tap target without growing
  the visible layout; PantryScreen's button also gained a missing
  type="button" and onMouseDown preventDefault.
- GUIDED/DETAILED GOAL-SETUP RELABEL + MERGED INFO CARD: the Create/edit
  goal flow's Simple/Custom segmented control is now labeled
  Guided/Detailed, with mode-specific copy ("We'll estimate your calories
  and macros..." / "Choose your own calorie and macro targets..."). Added
  an outline info card visually merged behind the segmented control
  (its top border crossing behind the pill's vertical center), reviving
  the round-65 design pattern. Follow-up fix: the segmented control was
  living in the sheet's sticky header, whose opaque background hid the
  info card's top border wherever it poked into that row — fixed by
  moving the segmented control out of the sticky header into normal
  scrolling flow (only the title now stays sticky). This also resolves
  the long-open "SHEET stickyHeader SLOT" HIGH gap from round 152/153,
  since that slot is no longer used for this control at all.
- TOP STATUS-BAR FADE GRADIENT: added a white gradient fade over the iOS
  notch/status-bar area app-wide (AppShell.tsx), mirroring the existing
  bottom-nav fade — pointer-events-none, sits on top, never affects
  content position; content scrolls through it unaffected, matching the
  "graceful — notch + extra runway" option Marco chose. Also experimented
  with an onTouchStart handler on the existing tap-to-scroll-top
  interceptor to fix a dead zone when tapping exactly on the clock;
  Marco reported it "didn't really work" but it's harmless and was left
  in place (see KNOWN GAPS — likely a WKWebView hit-testing limitation).
- KEYBOARD-COVERS-CONTENT BUG FIXED: several sheets were hiding form
  content behind the keyboard again. Root cause: OverlayLayer (used by
  "Edit food item" and other in-sheet overlays) was missing scroll-into-
  view logic entirely, unlike the main Sheet. Added the same Case-1/
  Case-2 focusin scroll-into-view effects to OverlayLayer, and corrected
  the keyboard-clearance math everywhere (Sheet.tsx, OverlayLayer,
  GoalSetupScreen.tsx, useKeyboardInset.ts's scrollFocusedAboveKeyboard)
  to add DONE_BAR_HEIGHT on top of the raw keyboard inset, since the
  Done bar sits on top of the keyboard rather than inside its reported
  height.
- SHEET HEADER SCROLL-SHADOW BUG FIXED (careful mode; resolves the open
  HIGH gap from round 152/153): Marco reported the "shadow casts when
  content scrolls under the sheet header" effect had regressed. Initial
  hypothesis (shadow too subtle to notice) was explicitly and firmly
  rejected by Marco ("there's a different issue at play, I'm 100% sure").
  Root-caused via an on-device debug readout Marco captured (temporarily
  added, then removed) showing scrolled=true even when no shadow was
  visible: a CSS stacking-order issue where a shadow cast by a normal-
  flow header can be silently painted over by a later sibling's own
  background unless the header has its own stacking context. Fixed with
  relative z-10 on both the main Sheet's header and OverlayLayer's nav
  bar. Cross-checked against the round-152/153 CHANGELOG history (a
  prior fix attempt caused the panel's rounded corners to go square) and
  confirmed this fix couldn't reproduce that regression, since it adds
  no new opaque background — only a stacking-context change.
  Also fixed a related bug surfaced by the same fix: OverlayLayer's
  scrolled state persists across different overlay screens (only the
  rendered content swaps), so a freshly-opened overlay (e.g. "Edit food
  item") could inherit scrolled: true from whatever was scrolled before
  it and show a stale shadow before any scrolling. Reset scrolled to
  false whenever OverlayLayer's node prop changes.
- OVERLAY SHADOW BLEEDING ONTO GRAB HANDLE FIXED: after the above fix,
  Marco spotted a new, related artifact — a faint shadow visible above
  OverlayLayer's nav row, over the grab handle, once scrolled. Root
  cause: shadow-nav's box-shadow (0 2px 8px -2px, src/index.css) has an
  8px blur radius that bleeds roughly that far above its own box's top
  edge. In the main Sheet, the grab handle lives inside the same
  shadow-casting box, so that bleed lands above the panel's visible
  content and gets clipped by the panel's own overflow-hidden/rounded
  top corner. In OverlayLayer the grab handle was a separate, earlier
  sibling sitting below that clipped zone, so the bleed was visible
  squarely between the grab handle and the nav row. Fixed by merging
  the grab handle and nav row into one shadow-casting box in
  OverlayLayer (same padding/margin math preserved), matching the main
  Sheet's structure so the bleed gets clipped the same way.
- GOAL FORK ENTRY ICON/TRANSITION FIXED: Marco asked for the "start a
  new goal" screen to show an X instead of a back arrow, and to slide up
  from the bottom instead of its current transition. First attempt
  incorrectly targeted GoalSetupScreen's details step (the wrong
  screen) and was fully reverted after Marco pointed out the actual
  screen was GoalForkScreen ("What's your goal?", /goal-fork). That
  screen already has the exact X-icon + slide-up-in treatment, gated
  behind a from=today query param already used by TodayScreen's two
  goal-fork entry points — GoalScreen's "Set a goal" button was calling
  nav('/goal-fork') with no param, landing in the back-arrow/no-
  animation branch instead. Fixed by reusing the existing from=today
  param on GoalScreen's button; no new code paths needed. GoalForkScreen
  is now device-reviewed and confirmed working (moved from a LOW "built
  but not device-reviewed" gap to DONE).
- tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 51 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).

ROUND 179 — SEED 150 DEFAULT PANTRY FOODS; LOCK THEIR MACROS (2026-07-05)
================================================================
Files: src/data/defaultPantry.ts (new), src/data/defaultPantrySeed.ts (new),
       src/data/defaultPantrySeed.test.ts (new), src/ui/AppShell.tsx,
       src/ui/components/FoodItemForm.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx, src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx,
       src/ui/kit/NumberField.tsx, src/ui/kit/LabeledInput.tsx,
       src/ui/kit/MeasurementTypeSelector.tsx

Follow-up to round 177's origin field/filter, which had nothing to filter
against yet — this actually populates the app side of "My own" vs default.

Conceptual discussion first (Marco wanted to think it through before any
code): settled on a ~120-150 food cap, no images for v1, free/bundled
data (no live API dependency), fixed/non-editable macros for app-provided
items, and no duplicate-detection UX beyond normal pantry naming — "if
the user wants their own, they just need their own name." Landed on 150
("a rounder number") after the initial 120 proposal.

Implementation:
- defaultPantry.ts: 150 common staples (proteins, grains, fruits,
  vegetables, dairy, fats/nuts/seeds, pantry basics), standard per-100g
  USDA-style macro figures. Stable hand-picked slug IDs (e.g.
  app-chicken-breast-cooked) rather than random UUIDs, specifically so a
  future update can add or correct entries without ever re-seeding or
  duplicating a row that's already on someone's device.
- defaultPantrySeed.ts: one-time migration mirroring mealsMigration.ts's
  own localStorage-flag-gated pattern (own flag key, own guarded runner +
  directly-testable inner function). Skips seeding any default whose name
  exactly matches (case-insensitive, trimmed — same rule the rest of the
  app already uses for pantry name uniqueness) an item a user already
  has, of either origin, so nobody ends up with two identically-named
  rows — their own item always wins, untouched. Wired into AppShell.tsx
  alongside the existing meals migration. 3 new tests: seed-from-empty,
  name-collision skip, and idempotency on a second run.
- FoodItemForm.tsx: origin:'app' items now lock measurement type and
  every macro field (calories/protein/carbs/fiber/fat) — name and photo
  stay editable — with a caption explaining why. Added `disabled` support
  to NumberField/LabeledInput and MeasurementTypeSelector to back this.
  Wired the new `origin` field through from every call site that edits an
  EXISTING item: PantryFoodItemDetail, PantryMealDetail, and
  AddEntrySheet's basket EditOverlay (via a lookup on the basket item's
  linked pantryItemId). Create-new-item call sites (pantry-new,
  meal-add-item, basket-manual) don't need it — nothing to lock yet.

Known, accepted trade-offs (see KNOWN GAPS): nutrition is computed live
from each Food item's current fields every time (nutritionFor in
calc.ts), never snapshotted at log time — so any future correction to a
seeded value would retroactively change historical diary entries for
everyone who logged it. Name-collision skip is exact-match only, not
fuzzy, so near-duplicate names (e.g. "Chicken Breast" vs the seeded
"Chicken breast, cooked") will both appear rather than merge. Macro data
was drawn from established nutrition-science figures, not verified
against a live USDA FoodData Central pull for each of the 150 items.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 51 passed (3 new), 8 skipped.
`npm run build` verified clean (+~17KB JS, no images — matches the
"should be free and super light" brief).

ROUND 177 — 3-PILL PANTRY FILTER: FOOD ITEMS / MEALS / MY OWN (2026-07-05)
================================================================
Files: src/domain/types.ts, src/ui/screens/PantryScreen.tsx

Marco wanted a filter that could eventually distinguish app-provided vs
user-added pantry items, alongside the existing Food item/Meal split.
Design discussion first: considered a tap-to-open-modal-per-filter
pattern (radio/checkbox lists), then simplified to 3 always-visible,
independently-toggleable pills reusing the exact look of FilterPills'
existing selected/unselected states:

- "Food items" and "Meals" union together — checking both, or neither,
  shows everything (same as the old single-select "All").
- "My own" is a separate, independent restriction to origin:'user' items,
  layered on top of whichever type(s) are showing — not a flat OR across
  all three pills. Meal rows always pass it through untouched, since
  every Meal is inherently user-made.
- "My own" is disabled and force-unchecked the moment the view enters a
  Meals-only state (from either direction — checking Meals off Food
  items, or unchecking Food items while Meals stays on), since there's no
  app-provided-Meal concept to filter by. Done inline in the two
  type-toggle handlers rather than a useEffect+setState, which the hooks
  linter flags as an unnecessary cascading-render pattern for what's
  really one user action with two state updates.

Built bespoke in PantryScreen rather than extending the shared
FilterPills component, since FilterPills' other 3 call sites
(PantryPicker, TodayScreen, GoalSetupScreen) are all single-select radio
and shouldn't inherit toggle/disabled behavior they don't need.

Also added an optional `origin?: 'app' | 'user'` field to FoodItem —
undefined on every existing item (always read as 'user', no migration
needed) — laying the groundwork for round 179's default-pantry seed;
nothing seeded 'app' yet at this point.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).
`npm run build` verified clean.

ROUND 171 — FIX APP-FREEZE BUG ON X -> DISCARD -> KEEP EDITING (2026-07-05)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/kit/Sheet.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx

Critical bug: tapping X on an already-open Meal/Food-item detail Sheet
visibly closed it, the discard-confirm Sheet appeared on top, and tapping
"Keep editing" left the entire app unresponsive. Took two attempts plus a
debug-logging pass to fix for real, after an earlier attempt looked
correct in code but was never actually confirmed on-device (a build never
got pushed before Marco re-tested it — an process mistake, acknowledged
mid-session).

Root cause: Sheet.tsx's close() function always played its animated
close regardless of caller intent — only the X button's own onClick
respected the `closeImmediately` prop; scrim-tap and swipe-dismiss always
animated. Combined with a second bug where lifting `hasChanges` via a
callback+useState pattern lagged behind live-query updates, the parent
Sheet would sometimes still be mid-close when the confirm Sheet tried to
reopen it, wedging the UI. Fix: close() now checks `closeImmediately`
itself before deciding whether to animate, and `closeImmediately` is
unconditionally `true` on both affected Sheets (PantryMealDetail,
PantryFoodItemDetail) — no lifted state, decision made synchronously and
fresh on every render. Verified via temporary debug logging read through
Safari Web Inspector against a real device build, then removed once
confirmed working.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).

ROUND 170 — 5 PANTRY BUGS; SESSION-CHANGES REVERT-ON-X; GREY-FILL CAP (2026-07-05)

Files: src/lib/useFillToBottom.ts, src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx

Grey-fill/white-gap follow-up to round 168's on-device confirmation pass:
useFillToBottom now takes a `bottomGapPx` parameter (36, combined with
24px of the panel's own padding for a ~60px total gap) so the grey panel
stretches to fill short content WITHOUT overshooting past a small, fixed
gap from the true bottom — long content is unaffected (its own trailing
padding already handles the gap). Applied to all 3 grey-panel call sites
(FoodForm, LogEntryContent, PantryMealDetail).

5 Pantry Meal/Food-item bugs fixed in the same pass: consistent 24px
spacing; hide the "Add another item" helper text and Save CTA in
PantryMealDetail when nothing's actually changed; X now cancels (not
saves) an in-progress name edit; removed a redundant "Close" button in
PantryFoodItemDetail; fixed a naming-uniqueness violation and an
eager-save bug when converting a Food item into a Meal via "add from
pantry" (now runs through uniquePantryName and is treated as

Escalated further mid-round: X on an already-saved Meal was only
guarding the name field — any other session change (item add/remove,
ingredient macro edits) still saved immediately regardless of Save vs X.
Added a full snapshot/revert mechanism: PantryMealDetailContent captures

derives hasChanges from it every render, and X now triggers

name/items and any changed ingredient fields) instead of a silent save —

measurementType/referenceAmount change, since that already triggered a
one-way cross-database unit conversion (convertFoodItemReferences) that

ROUND 169 — ACTIVITY-SCALED CARB TARGET MODEL (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/domain/goal.ts, src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/GoalSetupScreen.tsx

Marco flagged the Balanced/Performance carb target as way too high on a
rest day (340g carbs on a 420-active-kcal day) and shared a model from
another AI conversation: bucket carbs by active-calorie ranges. Dug into
the code first — the actual bug was that carbs were computed as a pure
residual, (totalBurn − protein_kcal − fat_kcal) ÷ 4, so almost the whole
BMR-driven calorie envelope landed in carbs regardless of activity.

Rather than a fixed lookup table (brittle — doesn't scale with the
person's actual weight/TDEE, needs new buckets if targets change),
implemented a continuous version: `activityCarbTargetG` in domain/goal.ts
computes a sedentary body-weight baseline (2.2g/kg Balanced, 2.4g/kg
Performance) plus a share of today's active calories converted to carb
grams (55%/75% of active kcal ÷ 4). Lower-carb is untouched — it already
has its own explicit, user-editable carbLimitG.

Wired into TodayScreen's live effectiveCarbLimit calc (uses today's real
activeKcal + current weight) and GoalSetupScreen's setup-time preview
(shows the resting baseline, activeKcal=0, same "Base X g · adjusts with
activity" copy now for BOTH Balanced and Performance — previously
Balanced showed no number at all since it had no real formula to show).

Sanity check at 70kg: 0 active kcal -> 154g (Balanced) / 168g
(Performance); 420 active kcal -> 212g / 247g; 1000 active kcal -> 292g
/ 356g — night-and-day difference from the old 340g-on-a-rest-day result.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).
`npm run build` verified clean.

ROUND 168 — GREY-FILL ROOT-CAUSE FIX, NEW PLACEHOLDER, 32PX RADIUS, SPACING (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/lib/useFillToBottom.ts, src/assets/food-placeholder.png (replaced),
       src/ui/kit/ImageHero.tsx, src/ui/components/FoodItemForm.tsx,
       src/ui/components/MethodCards.tsx, src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx

1. Found the actual root cause of the grey-panel-fill bug round 167's
   useFillToBottom hook didn't fix: Sheet.tsx slides the panel up over
   OPEN_MS = 420ms (a CSS transform transition), but the hook's re-measure
   timeouts (50ms, 350ms) both fired BEFORE that transition finished —
   capturing a `top` value from mid-animation (higher up than the panel's
   resting position), which undersized the computed fill and left the
   exact plain-white gap Marco kept reporting ("still nothing... the
   bottom area is white"). Rewrote the hook to poll across the whole
   settle window (50/120/200/300/420/520/650/850ms) and also re-measure
   on every `transitionend` bubbling up from anywhere in the document, so
   it no longer depends on guessing one "safe" delay.

2. Swapped in Marco's new food_item_placeholder_2.png (569KB) as the
   default placeholder, replacing the previous food-placeholder.png
   (812KB) — same filename/import path in ImageHero and FoodItemForm, so
   no code changes needed beyond the asset itself. Deleted both the old
   food-placeholder.png and the interim 1.5MB upload from the repo.

3. Image corner radius: 20px -> 32px everywhere a photo or placeholder
   renders (ImageHero's single/collage/placeholder boxes, FoodItemForm's
   with-photo and add-photo boxes) — now matching radius-main (32px),
   the same radius already used by the Meal-summary card these images
   sit inside.

4. AddAnotherSection: 16px corner radius while collapsed, the existing
   24px once expanded.

5. Day's-log section: bottom padding 12px -> 8px (TodayScreen.tsx).

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).
`npm run build` verified clean.

ROUND 167 — FIX GREY-FILL BUG (JS-MEASURED), DARKER BORDER, EMPTY-FAB IMAGE, ADD-PHOTO CTA (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/lib/useFillToBottom.ts (new), src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx (FoodForm, LogEntryContent),
       src/ui/components/MethodCards.tsx, src/ui/components/FoodItemForm.tsx

1. "the grey background is not going all the way to the end... i'm afraid
   not, the problem still persists." Round 166's CSS-only fix (flex
   flex-col + min-height:100% on the outer wrapper, flex:1 on the grey
   panel) didn't hold up on-device. Replaced it with a JS-measured
   approach: a new `useFillToBottom` hook (src/lib/useFillToBottom.ts)
   reads the panel's actual `getBoundingClientRect().top` and returns
   `window.innerHeight - top` as a min-height in px, re-measuring on
   resize and shortly after mount (for the sheet's slide-up animation and
   any late image loads). This sidesteps the whole percentage/flexbox
   resolution question entirely by computing the exact remaining space
   directly. Wired into all 3 Basket Meal views (PantryMealDetail,
   FoodForm, LogEntryContent) in place of the old flex:1 grey wrapper.
   Caught and fixed a Rules-of-Hooks ordering bug along the way in both
   PantryMealDetail and FoodForm — each has an early-return spinner state
   (analysing/committing) above where the hook needed to live, so the
   call was relocated up to sit alongside each component's other
   top-level hooks, unconditionally before any early return.

2. "can you please go to the next darkest border color please" (for the
   Meal-only "+Add a new food item" border from round 166). Swapped
   AddAnotherSection's `bordered` styling from border-border-field to
   border-border-strong, the next step down in the light-theme border
   scale.

3. "for the empty sheet, so when i tap on the fab button, it shouldn't
   have any image." FoodForm's ImageHero was showing (placeholder or
   real) whenever the basket had 0 or 1 items; changed the condition to
   only show it once there's exactly 1 item, so a freshly-opened FAB with
   nothing picked yet shows no image slot at all.

4. "the modal sheet edit view of a food item, we have a CTA saying 'add
   photo' with a camera icon. could we change this CTA to match the
   actual photo. 256x256." Replaced FoodItemForm's thin outlined
   "Add photo" button with a 256x256 box using the same food-placeholder
   image and rounded-[20px] treatment as the real-photo state (no shadow,
   matching the placeholder spec), with a centered "Add photo" pill
   overlay (camera icon + label) so it stays discoverable as tappable —
   the same overlay pattern the with-photo state already uses for
   "Change photo."

Also answered two factual questions in chat (no code changes): image
corner radius is 20px (rounded-[20px]) everywhere ImageHero/FoodItemForm
render a photo or placeholder; on the Diary screen, vertical spacing from
the screen top to the gauge card is 4px (mt-1), and from the gauge/macro
section to the Day's-log section is 16px (mt-4) when no weight-reminder
banner is showing (the banner inserts itself between them first when
present).

tsc: clean. lint: clean (one eslint-disable-line react-hooks/refs added
in PantryMealDetail for the new grey-fill div — a pre-existing
during-render ref write earlier in that same component trips the
analyzer's conservative ref-taint tracking for unrelated ref/state reads
later in render; same suppression pattern already used elsewhere in this
file). vitest: 48 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged). `npm run build` verified
clean.

ROUND 163 — MEAL-CARD LAYERING REBUILT ON THE GAUGE-CARD PATTERN + DEFAULT IMAGE PLACEHOLDER (2026-07-04)

image" — ImageHero previously returned null for an empty photos array;

shadow) at the same 256×256 size instead, per Marco's "flat muted grey
and no shadow" spec for the still-pending default-image asset. The two
call sites that gated ImageHero behind `photos.length > 0 &&` (FoodForm,
LogEntryContent) now call it unconditionally, same as PantryMealDetail
and PantryFoodItemDetail already did. Real photos also dropped their own
shadow-card-lg (single hero shot and the 2-4 photo collage tiles alike) —
now that the image is nested inside the meal card's own shadow, keeping
a second shadow on the photo itself would double up; matches how
GaugeArc has no shadow of its own inside the gauge card either.

ROUND 162 — LAYERED MEAL-CARD HIERARCHY ACROSS ALL 3 BASKET MEAL VIEWS (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx, src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx
       (LogEntryContent, FoodForm)

Marco's new design concept: create clearer visual hierarchy between the
meal-summary card and its contents by making the card look like it's
floating ON TOP of the "Food items" section below, instead of the two
reading as separate, similarly-weighted white boxes on the same
background. Confirmed via follow-up questions:
  - Individual food-item cards (BasketCard/PantryItemCard) keep their
    existing white shadow-card styling, unchanged.
  - The grey section is UNDER the meal card, its rounded top corners
    peeking out from behind/around the card rather than starting flush
    below it.
  - Grey bleeds edge-to-edge (full sheet width), not just card-width.
  - Applies to all 3 Basket Meal views: PantryMealDetail, LogEntryContent
    (Day's-log edit Meal), FoodForm (FAB fresh-capture Meal results).

Actually inverted what was backwards before: the meal-summary card
previously had NO shadow (round 152/153 — "outline only"), while the
item cards below it already had shadow-card — so the children looked
more elevated than their own parent. Now: the meal card gets
border-border-subtle + shadow-card + `relative` (round 152's
border-card-no-shadow removed here specifically); everything under it —
"Food items" heading, item list, "Add a new food item", the Log/Save
CTA — moves into one new rounded-t-sheet bg-surface-sunken panel that:
  - bleeds past the Sheet's own 20px side padding (negative left/right
    margin, padding reapplied on its content so nothing touches the
    screen edges),
  - pulls up 16px under the card's bottom edge (negative top margin) so
    it appears to run behind the card instead of starting right after it,
  - keeps the established 24px card-to-heading gap via paddingTop = 16px
    (the overlap) + 24px.
The card being `relative` (positioned) is what makes it paint above the
plain, non-positioned grey panel in that overlapping band — no z-index
needed, just CSS's own positioned-vs-static paint order.

LogEntryContent and FoodForm both restructured their basket.length>=2 /
<2 branching so "Add a new food item"/CTA render once (as a shared JSX
value placed inside the grey panel for 2+ items, or below the single
item's card unchanged for 1 item) instead of duplicating that JSX.
PantryMealDetail's version is unconditional (a Meal is always 2+ items
structurally), so no branching was needed there.

Built without on-device visual verification (no headless-browser preview
available in this environment) — overlap amount (16px), corner radius
(rounded-t-sheet, the existing 24px "big surfaces" token), and grey shade
(bg-surface-sunken) are first-pass, reasoned choices, not pixel-confirmed.
Flagged to Marco to review on his phone and give exact adjustments.

ROUND 160 — NUMERIC-KEYBOARD "DONE" BAR PROMOTED APP-WIDE (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/kit/useNumericDoneBar.tsx (new), src/ui/kit/NumberField.tsx,
       src/ui/kit/index.ts, src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/GoalSetupScreen.tsx, src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/AccountScreen.tsx

Marco: "let's then swap or update all instances of the keyboard with
numbers only by the new one please" — referring to option 4 from round
158/159's Dev > Keyboards playground (decimal keypad + a custom "Done"
bar sliding in above the keyboard on focus), which he'd already confirmed
as the one he wants.

Extracted that pattern out of DevMenu.tsx into a reusable kit hook,
`useNumericDoneBar()` (src/ui/kit/useNumericDoneBar.tsx): tracks whether
a bound input is focused via `bind` (onFocus/onBlur, composable with a
field's own handlers), and renders a fixed, keyboard-pinned "Done" bar
(via the same useKeyboardInset() Sheet.tsx already uses) that blurs
whichever input is currently focused when tapped — same onMouseDown +
preventDefault trick as before, since onMouseDown fires before onBlur on
iOS/WebKit.

Applied it everywhere a numeric keyboard appears in the real app (left
the Dev > Pickers/Keyboards playgrounds and the Shadow-playground/
Styleguide dev-only number inputs alone, since those are deliberately
side-by-side comparisons, not production fields):

- NumberField (src/ui/kit/NumberField.tsx) — the app's shared decimal
  input, already used by FoodItemForm's Serving size/Calories/Protein/
  Carbs/Fat/Fiber and AddEntrySheet's scan-result editor fields. Now also
  carries lang="en-US" (round 159's "," -> "." fix) and gained an
  optional `placeholder` prop (previously dropped silently) to preserve
  the two fields below that relied on one.
- Day's-log basket quantity stepper's tap-to-type input (BasketStepper in
  AddEntrySheet.tsx) — kept its own inline input (doesn't fit NumberField's
  labeled-field shape) but wired the same hook directly, plus lang="en-US".
- GoalSetupScreen's Height/Age fields — previously raw LabeledInput +
  inputMode="numeric"; converted to NumberField outright.
- Activity "Calories" field — previously raw LabeledInput + type="number"
  in BOTH TodayScreen's edit-activity sheet and AddEntrySheet's manual-add
  form; both converted to NumberField.
- AccountScreen's protein-target field — previously raw LabeledInput +
  inputMode="numeric"; converted to NumberField.

Net effect: one shared numeric-input implementation for the whole app
(instead of ~6 hand-rolled variants), and every numeric keyboard now has
a way to close itself, closing the "no closing action" half of the
long-standing KEYBOARD TOOLBAR known gap (prev/next field navigation
between inputs remains open).

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).
`npm run build` verified clean.

Also this round: Day's-log Activity list-item row had its name/kcal weights
backwards relative to the Food row convention (established round 152 — name
bold, amount regular). Swapped: activity name is now font-bold, kcal is now
regular weight, matching the Food row exactly (src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx).

ROUND 158 — DEV: KEYBOARD PLAYGROUND (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/DevMenu.tsx

New "Keyboards" section in Account > Developers, mirroring the existing
"Native pickers" section / Picker playground pattern exactly (same
PickerRow/numRange helpers reused). Opens a "Keyboard playground" Sheet
with 4 rows, all starting at a number value, to compare on-device:
  1. inputMode="numeric" — whole-number keypad, no decimal key, no Return.
  2. inputMode="decimal" — the app's current everywhere-used approach
     (NumberField); adds a decimal point key, still no Return.
  3. type="tel" — phone keypad, has a stray "#" key, still no Return.
  4. inputMode="decimal" + a custom "Done" toolbar that slides in above the
     keyboard on focus, addressing Marco's ask for a numeric keyboard with
     a closing/confirming action — iOS's native number pad has no Return
     key at all, so this is the standard web/hybrid workaround. Built with
     the SAME useKeyboardInset() hook Sheet.tsx already uses (Capacitor
     Keyboard plugin on device — exact height regardless of resize mode),
     so the bar sits flush against the real keyboard top rather than
     guessing a fixed offset. Uses onMouseDown (not onClick) + preventDefault
     on the Done button, since onMouseDown fires before the input's onBlur
     on iOS/WebKit — without it the blur can race and dismiss oddly.

This is a working reference for the long-standing "KEYBOARD TOOLBAR"
Known Gap (prev/next field nav + dismiss button above the keyboard) —
row 4 demonstrates the exact mechanism that gap would use.

ROUND 157 — DISCARD-ON-X FOR SCAN RESULTS, SPACING PARITY, FAB MEAL-CARD PARITY (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx

Four items from one round of feedback:

1. IMAGE-TO-CONTAINER SPACING PARITY for single Food items. The 24px gap
   established for the Meal-summary card (rounds 152-156) hadn't reached
   the single-item views — Marco flagged both LogEntryContent's own "Food
   item" edit view (Day's-log) and PantryFoodItemDetail (Pantry) as still
   showing the old, larger, ambient space-y-driven gap. Both now wrap their
   item card in `style={{ marginTop: '24px' }}`, same technique as the Meal
   case. Scoped to just these two Food-item surfaces per Marco's "only for
   the food items."

2. "+ NEW FOOD": X NO LONGER KEEPS THE SCAN RESULT. Since round 149,
   Camera/Photo/Describe/Nutri-scan commit straight to the DB the instant a
   result comes back — there's no separate "confirm" step, the landing Food
   item / Meal detail IS the confirmation. But that meant tapping the X on
   that landing screen still left the item/meal sitting in the Pantry,
   since it was already written before the user ever saw it. Rather than
   rearchitect into a true "draft, not yet persisted" mode (a much larger
   change touching every sub-flow of these two already-complex detail
   screens), took the simpler, equally-correct route: X on a freshly
   scan-created item/meal (`justCreated` / `justCreatedItemIds`) now shows
   a "Discard this food/meal?" confirm Sheet instead of closing directly;
   confirming actually deletes the just-written record
   (repos.foodItems.remove / repos.meals.remove) before closing. The header
   delete (trash) button is hidden in this state too, since X now serves
   that role. "Save food"/"Save meal" need no change — the record's already
   correctly saved, tapping them just closes as before. A Meal ingredient
   set by a "Change" correction (round 151) that expands 1 item into 2+ now
   also passes its new ingredient ids through onMealCreated, so that
   resulting Meal is equally eligible for both this discard-confirm and the
   existing "Change" treatment — it was previously falling through that
   gap since it arrived via a correction rather than a fresh capture.

3. NAME-CONFLICT ERROR WHEN EDITING AN ALREADY-SAVED ITEM — investigated,
   not a code bug. Both "Edit food item" overlays (PantryFoodItemDetail's
   own item, and PantryMealDetail's per-ingredient edit) already pass
   existingItemId correctly through to findPantryNameConflict, which
   explicitly excludes that id from the conflict check. If the warning
   still appears while editing without renaming, it means there's a
   genuinely different existing item/meal sharing that exact name already
   in the pantry — most likely a leftover duplicate from before round 152's
   uniquePantryName fix (which only prevents FUTURE duplicates, not existing
   ones). Flagged back to Marco to check for a second item/meal with the
   same name rather than changing logic that's already correct.

4. FAB → CAMERA "MEAL RESULTS" STILL SHOWED THE OLD CARD. The FAB's fresh
   scan flow uses FoodForm (its own component, separate from
   LogEntryContent, which only handles editing an ALREADY-LOGGED entry) —
   rounds 152-156's meal-summary-card work never touched it. Applied the
   identical treatment here too: outlined summary card (name + total kcal +
   macro badges + "Save to pantry"), "Food items" heading + spacing, and
   the same 24px single-item gap from point 1.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).
`npm run build` verified clean.

ROUND 154 — MACRO BADGE SIZE, MEAL-CARD BORDER TOKEN, EXACT PIXEL SPACING (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/kit/MacroSummaryLine.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx

Second follow-up round on the meal-summary/macro-badge work:

- Macro badges: text stepped down one notch in the type scale, from
  text-subhead (15px) to text-footnote (13px).

- Meal summary card border: border-border-subtle -> border-border-card-no-
  shadow. There's no literal "border-hover" token in this design system
  (checked index.css's @theme block) — border-card-no-shadow is the one
  explicitly documented for "flat card containers with no shadow", which is
  exactly this card since round 153 dropped its shadow. In light AND dark
  mode this resolves to the identical value as border-field, so visually
  it's the same either way.

- Kcal badge background (next to the meal-name field) changed from
  bg-surface to bg-surface-sunken, matching the meal-name input's own
  background, per Marco's ask that the two containers match.

- Spacing, made exact and collapse-proof: Marco reported the rendered gap
  between the meal photo and the summary card was only ~4px despite the
  space-y utility supposedly giving 16-24px — and that the two views (Day's-
  log / Pantry) weren't even consistent with each other (space-y-4 vs
  space-y-3). Rather than keep reasoning about it from source, replaced
  ambient space-y-driven spacing around these specific elements with
  explicit inline styles, which unambiguously win over any conflicting
  class and can't be silently eaten by margin collapsing: the summary
  card now has `style={{ marginTop: '24px' }}` directly (image -> card gap,
  identical in both views); "Food items" and its item list now share a
  small wrapper div so the heading's own `marginBottom: '8px'` faces a
  first-child item card with no competing auto margin-top of its own,
  guaranteeing the requested 24px-top / 8px-bottom around the label exactly
  as rendered, not just as authored. The inner wrapper reproduces the same
  gap the item cards always had between each other (space-y-4 in Pantry,
  space-y-3 in Day's-log) — that spacing is unchanged, only the boundary
  around the "Food items" label itself is now pinned.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).
`npm run build` verified clean.

ROUND 152 — SIX-PART UI/UX BATCH: BADGES, BOLD NAMES, MEAL SUMMARY CARD, SHADOWS, DUPES (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/domain/pantry.ts, src/ui/kit/MacroSummaryLine.tsx, src/ui/kit/Sheet.tsx,
       src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryItemCard.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryPicker.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryNewFoodScan.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/PantryScreen.tsx, src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx

Six independent fixes/changes from one round of feedback, validated with Marco
point-by-point before implementing:

1. "+ New food" method cards moved up 16px (helper-text bottom padding
   pb-6 -> pb-2) with a 16px spacer added after the cards so the sheet's
   total height is unchanged — Marco wanted the shift purely visual, not a
   net resize.

2. MacroSummaryLine (shared by BasketCard and PantryItemCard) changed from
   one long "Protein 3.5g · Carbs 30g · Fibre 1g · Fat 6g" pill to four
   individual badges, each `px-2 py-0.5` per Marco's exact spec.

3. Food name now bold / kcal now regular weight (previously the reverse)
   across every card that pairs the two: Basket card, Pantry item card, the
   Pantry list row, the Add-from-pantry picker rows (both the Pantry-side
   PantryPicker and the Day's-log basket's own inline pantry-search rows),
   and the Diary's own per-entry log row.

4. Meal nutrition summary — the long-standing TODO (flagged since round
   123). Both places that show a Meal's own item list now open with a
   summary card wrapping the meal name, its total kcal (disabled-looking
   badge beside the name field), and a macro breakdown, followed by a
   "Food items" section label before the per-item cards:
     - Day's-log's "Meal" view (LogEntrySheet/LogEntryContent, editing an
       already-logged meal entry) — this one ALSO gained a date subtitle
       on the Sheet header (calendar icon + the entry's own date, same
       badge styling as the main "Add" sheet's dateSubtitle) since it was
       missing one entirely.
     - Pantry's own Meal detail (PantryMealDetail) — same summary-card
       treatment, minus the "Save to pantry" checkbox (a Pantry Meal
       already IS a pantry object) and minus any date (a reusable template
       has none — Marco was explicit this stays exactly as-is, no date
       added here).

5. Two scroll-shadow bugs in Sheet.tsx:
     - The main Sheet's own header shadow was disappearing once scrolled
       content reached the top and touched it. Cause: the header wrapper
       had no background of its own (just showing the panel's bg-surface
       through it) — later-painted, opaque scrolled content covered the
       shadow bleeding into that space. Fixed by giving the header wrapper
       its own explicit `bg-surface`.
     - OverlayLayer's nav bar (used by DescribeOverlay, "Edit food item",
       etc.) had a stray shadow appearing to sit ABOVE the nav bar. Cause:
       a `-mt-1` negative margin pulled the nav up to overlap the grab
       handle above it, and since box-shadow blurs in every direction (not
       just downward), that overlap showed the shadow bleeding onto the
       handle. Removed the negative margin.

6. Duplicate pantry names from repeat scans. Manual entry already blocks
   Save on a name collision via findPantryNameConflict, but the AI-capture
   commit paths (round 149's "commit immediately," no form to show an
   inline error in) had no equivalent check — scanning the same photo
   repeatedly (deterministic AI naming) silently created several Food
   items or Meals all named identically. Added `uniquePantryName()` to
   domain/pantry.ts (auto-suffixes "(2)", "(3)"...) and wired it into every
   scan-driven Food-item/Meal creation path: both branches of PantryNewFood's
   commitCaptured, and PantryFoodItemDetail's commitScannedItems +
   handleDescribeAnalyzeForCorrection. Deliberately NOT applied to Manual/
   pick-existing-item/pick-existing-meal paths in the same files — those
   aren't what Marco reported, and already involve an explicit user action
   each time rather than a silently-repeatable scan.

ROUND 151 — BRING BACK THE "CHANGE" BUTTON ON SCAN-CAPTURED PANTRY ITEMS (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/PantryItemCard.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx, src/ui/screens/PantryScreen.tsx

Marco: "we're missing again the 'Change' button for photo taken, photo from
library." Round 148 added a "Change" button (re-run Describe to replace a
card's macros) to the Pantry scan flows' pre-commit review screen — but
round 149, immediately after in the same session, removed that whole review
screen since items now commit straight through with no review step, taking
the Change button down with it (documented at the time as a deliberate,
if unfortunate, side effect — "there's no longer a pre-commit card to
attach it to"). Marco wants the affordance back.

The button itself was never meant to be permanent — even in its original
home (the Day's-log basket's BasketCard), it only ever existed for
uncommitted items sitting in the in-progress basket array, gone the moment
you saved. The equivalent "moment" in the now-commit-immediately Pantry
flows is: right after landing on the Food item / Meal detail a scan just
produced, for as long as that Sheet stays open.

Added `onCorrect` to PantryItemCard (same pill button/copy as BasketCard's).
PantryFoodItemDetail shows it on its own item card exactly when `justCreated`
is true (round 150's flag) — tapping it opens Describe in a new
"describe-correct" overlay mode that overwrites this exact FoodItem's fields
in place (same id, so nothing else needs to change). If the correction
comes back with 2+ foods instead of 1 (you described more than what's
actually one item), it converts to a Meal instead — same 1-vs-2+ rule as
every other commit path — and deletes the original single item, which is
safe because nothing could have referenced it yet.

PantryMealDetail tracks a `scannedItemIds` set (which ingredient FoodItem
ids are still correctable): seeded from a new `justCreatedItemIds` prop
when the whole Meal just arrived from a scan ("+ New food" producing 2+
items, or Food item detail's "Create a meal" section — the latter only
marks the NEWLY captured ingredients, not the pre-existing item the meal
was built around), and grown further as more items get scanned in via
"Add another item" while the Sheet stays open. "Change" on one of these
splices whatever Describe returns (could be 1 or several foods) into
meal.items in place of that one ingredient; the superseded FoodItem is left
orphaned rather than deleted, same as removing an item from a meal already
does today.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).
`npm run build` verified clean.
