action sheet. Root cause: PantryScreen tracked `pickingMethod` (renders
MethodPickerModal, its own Sheet) and `scanMethod` (renders the old
PantryNewFoodScan, ALSO its own forceExpanded Sheet) as two separate pieces
of state — tapping a method closed one Sheet and opened a different one a
frame later, which read as a flash of an empty full-height sheet.

Fixed by collapsing the whole "+ New food" flow into ONE component
(`PantryNewFood`, replacing both `MethodPickerModal` usage and the old
`PantryNewFoodScan`) that renders a SINGLE, compact (non-forceExpanded)
Sheet for its entire lifetime, swapping only its inner content: method
picker -> analyzing spinner / Describe textarea -> commit. PantryScreen now
tracks one `newFoodOpen` boolean instead of `pickingMethod` + `scanMethod`.
Manual is unaffected — it still hands off to its own separate, forceExpanded
form Sheet, the one case that genuinely needs the extra room to fill in
macros.

One deliberate deviation from the meal-builder surfaces: Describe's textarea
is rendered as a plain child of this Sheet rather than via
`useSheetSetOverlay` (the right-slide-in panel mechanism PantryMealDetail/
PantryFoodItemDetail use for the same DescribeOverlay). Checked every other
call site in the app (`grep -rln useSheetSetOverlay src/ui`) and every one
of them pairs it with `forceExpanded` — nothing in the codebase exercises
that overlay mechanism on a natural-height Sheet, and this Sheet needs to
stay compact per Marco's ask. DescribeOverlay's own `OverlayNav`/
`useOverlaySetFooter` calls no-op safely without that context (verified by
reading their implementations — both read a context that defaults to null
outside `OverlayLayer`), so the only visible difference is losing the
"< Describe" sub-header row; the Sheet's own "New food" title + X already
covered "cancel out of this" before (Describe's back arrow already just
called `onClose`, closing the whole flow — no regression there).

Also: Marco noted the CTA on landing after a scan should be a primary
"Save food", not the outline "Close" — because unlike Manual (which already
had an explicit "Save food" tap before landing on the detail view), the AI
methods commit straight through with no confirm step (round 149), so the
detail view IS the implicit confirmation. Added a `justCreated` prop to
PantryFoodItemDetail, set only when `onFoodCreated` fires from a scan (not
from Manual's `handleCreateFood`, which already had its own explicit save).
PantryMealDetail needed no change — its CTA was already an unconditional
primary "Save meal" regardless of how the meal was reached.

NOT done this round (needs Marco's input before touching it): his "funny
use case" observation that Nutri-scan also successfully reads whole food
(not just packaged nutrition labels), which makes always asking "per 100g
or per serving?" there — while Camera/Photo/Describe never ask it — feel
inconsistent. His proposed rule ("ask only if it's scanning a nutrition
sheet") isn't mechanically detectable from the current scanFood API
response (label vs. whole-food photos both come back as a single item with
name/estimatedGrams/macros — no signal distinguishing them). Flagged to
Marco with two concrete options rather than guessing; see TODO section.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged).
`npm run build` verified clean.

ROUND 149 — PANTRY SCAN METHODS: DROP THE REVIEW SCREEN, COMMIT IMMEDIATELY (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryNewFoodScan.tsx, src/ui/components/FoodCapture.tsx

Marco caught this with a flow diagram: rounds 145/147/148's Camera/Photo/

already works: Manual's "Save & add to meal" and Add-from-pantry's picker

resulting Meal/Food item detail. No intermediate review list, no separate
confirm button. Correcting a wrong AI read now happens the same way as
correcting any other meal item: tap it afterward to edit, using the

ROUND 146 — STALE NATIVE BUNDLE, TAKE 2: NATIVE-LAYER CACHE CLEAR (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: ios/App/App/AppDelegate.swift

Round 145's fix (stop registering the PWA service worker on native, self-
unregister from main.tsx) turned out not to be reliably enough on its own:
once a service worker is ALREADY controlling the WKWebView (from an earlier
build, before round 145), it can keep serving its OWN precached bundle
indefinitely — including on every subsequent app relaunch — without ever
giving the NEW build's JS (which contains the unregister-itself code) an
actual chance to run. A pure-JS self-heal depends on WebKit's own service-
worker update-check timing, which isn't guaranteed to happen promptly for
locally-bundled Capacitor content, and evidently didn't here even after a
real Xcode rebuild + a few relaunches (Marco: "still not working").

Fixed at the native layer instead, which sidesteps the chicken-and-egg
problem entirely: AppDelegate's didFinishLaunchingWithOptions now clears
WKWebsiteDataStore's ServiceWorkerRegistrations + FetchCache (the Cache
Storage API Workbox precaches into) + Disk/Memory/OfflineWebApplication
caches, BEFORE the web view ever loads a page — so there's no cached service
worker left to decide what to serve in the first place. Runs on every
launch (cheap no-op once already clean, so no downside to leaving it in
permanently). Deliberately does NOT touch IndexedDB, LocalStorage,
SessionStorage, or Cookies — none of the real app data (goals, foods,
weigh-ins) lives in any of the cleared types, only in IndexedDB.

Not verified compiling in Xcode from this session (no Mac/Xcode toolchain
available in the Cowork sandbox) — this is a small, standard WKWebsiteDataStore
API call, but flag any build error from this file immediately so it can be
fixed right away.

================================================================
ROUND 145 — PANTRY MEAL BUILDER: CAMERA/PHOTO/DESCRIBE/NUTRI-SCAN + STALE NATIVE BUNDLE FIX (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/lib/foodScan.ts, src/ui/kit/FloatingTabBar.tsx,
       src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx, src/ui/components/MethodCards.tsx,
       src/ui/components/basketHelpers.ts (NEW), src/ui/components/useFoodCapture.tsx (NEW),
       src/ui/components/FoodCapture.tsx (NEW), src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx, vite.config.ts, src/main.tsx

Closed the last piece of the meals-in-pantry Known Gap: Camera/Photo/Describe/
Nutri-scan in the Pantry meal builder ("Create a meal" / "Add a new food
item") no longer toast "coming soon" — they reuse the exact same scan/
describe/label-scan mechanics, copy, and error handling as the Day's-log
basket, rather than re-deriving them.

── EXTRACTION (Pantry side reuses Day's-log's own scan/describe engine) ─────
Extracted the mechanics (runScan/handleCamera/handlePhoto/
handleDescribeAnalyze/handleLabelScan — verbatim copies of AddEntrySheet.tsx's
existing logic, no behaviour changes) into a new `useFoodCapture()` hook,
generalised behind an `onCaptured(items, source?)` callback so any surface can
plug in its own basket. BasketItem + its pure helpers (basketNutrition,
cleanScanName, scanResultToBasket, pantryToBasket) moved to the new
basketHelpers.ts (kept out of component files — mixing a hook/plain-function
export with component exports trips eslint-plugin-react-refresh's
only-export-components rule). BasketCard/DescribeOverlay/EditOverlay/
ServingModal are now exported from AddEntrySheet.tsx instead of being copied
a third time; a new FoodCapture.tsx holds AnalyzingIndicator + the new
CaptureReviewOverlay (built from BasketCard) for the review-before-commit
step. FoodForm/LogEntryContent's OWN scan logic is deliberately left
untouched (still has its pre-existing internal duplication) — not risking a
rushed refactor of Marco's live daily-use flow, same reasoning as round 122's
BasketCard/PantryItemCard split.

── PANTRY WIRING ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
PantryMealDetail / PantryFoodItemDetail: Camera/Photo call the capture hook
directly (native camera / web file-input fallback, same SCAN_ENABLED gate);
Describe opens the shared DescribeOverlay; Nutri-scan opens the shared label
file picker. Results land in a local review list (reusing BasketCard: qty
stepper, remove, edit) before being committed — each captured item becomes a
real Food item with `isArchived: true` by default (matches round 130/144's
hidden-ingredient rule), opt-out via the per-item "Save to pantry" checkbox on
the reused EditOverlay (which also lets the item's photo/macros be corrected
before committing). PantryMealDetail appends the new items to the existing
Meal ("Add to meal"); PantryFoodItemDetail creates a brand-new Meal combining
the existing item + the captured ones ("Create meal"), mirroring
handleAddItem/handleAddMealItem's existing semantics exactly.

Scope note: PantryScreen's "+ New food" Camera/Photo/Describe/Nutri-scan is
intentionally still "coming soon" — scanning can return multiple items, which
doesn't fit that flow's single-item model without a product decision first
(pick one of several? create a Meal instead?). Left for Marco to decide,
tracked in Known Gaps.

── SEPARATE BUG: STALE NATIVE BUNDLE AFTER ios:sync ─────────────────────────
Found while verifying on-device: after `npm run ios:sync` + a real Xcode
rebuild/run, the app kept showing pre-round-145 behaviour. Root cause: a
leftover PWA service worker (registered by an earlier install) was still
serving its OWN stale cached bundle from Cache Storage — exactly the failure
mode INSTALL-iOS.md's troubleshooting section already flagged as a known
risk, now actually hit. The service worker is only meant for the browser/PWA
install path, never the native (Capacitor) build.
Fixed: vite.config.ts sets `injectRegister: false` so vite-plugin-pwa no
longer auto-injects its unconditional `<script>` that registered the SW on
every load, regardless of platform. main.tsx now registers the service
worker ONLY on the browser/PWA path (`!Capacitor.isNativePlatform()`); on
native it instead actively unregisters any existing service worker
registrations and clears all Cache Storage on every launch — self-healing
for installs already affected by the bug, no app deletion needed. This does
NOT touch IndexedDB, where the real app data (goals, foods, weigh-ins) lives
— only the service worker's own cache.
Also updated INSTALL-iOS.md's troubleshooting entry to reflect the fix.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed, 8 skipped (unchanged). `npm run
build` verified clean — confirmed the built index.html no longer contains
the registerSW.js script tag.

ROUND 143 — "SAVE TO PANTRY" CHECKBOX STUCK ON AFTER DELETING THE MEAL (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx

Marco: scan a photo → get a Meal → save to Pantry. Delete that Meal from
Pantry. Reopen the same entry from Day's log — the "Save to pantry"
checkbox still shows checked, even though the Meal no longer exists.
Ticking it again does nothing (it's already "checked"), so there's no way
to re-add it to Pantry short of rebuilding the whole meal from scratch.

Root cause: `useState(!!entry.mealId)` initialized the checkbox purely
from whether the ENTRY has a mealId field at all — it never checked
whether a Meal with that id still actually exists. Deleting a Meal from
Pantry doesn't (and shouldn't) go back and clear mealId off every entry
that ever logged it, so the checkbox was reading a stale reference as
"still linked" forever after.

Fixed: `saveToPantry` is now an override layered on top of a live-derived
default (`mealStillExists = meals.some(m => m.id === entry.mealId)`), not
raw state seeded once at mount — same "derived value, not setState-in-
effect" approach as round 135's unlink checkbox. A deleted Meal now shows
the checkbox correctly OFF; ticking it back on and saving recreates the
Meal (repos.meals.put reuses the same, now-free, id — a normal upsert).

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed (unchanged — UI-state fix, no
new domain logic). `npm run build` verified clean.

ROUND 142 — REVERT ROUND 141's IMAGEHERO EXPERIMENT (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/kit/ImageHero.tsx

Marco tested round 141's photo-brightness experiment (rounding moved from
a wrapping overflow-hidden div onto the <img> itself): no change to the
brightness, AND it introduced a real regression — square white corners on
the Day's-log "basket" photo preview (ImageHero is shared by both the
Pantry Food item detail and the Day's-log basket photo). Root cause:
border-radius directly on an `object-fit: cover` <img> doesn't reliably
clip in WebKit — a known engine quirk, distinct from the compositing
theory round 141 was testing. Reverted to the original overflow-hidden
wrapper pattern (matches FoodItemForm's photo editor, which was never
touched and never had this problem).

This also DISPROVES round 141's "GPU compositing layer" theory outright:
Marco separately reported that FoodItemForm's edit-photo view — which
still uses overflow-hidden + rounded corners, unchanged this whole time —
displays the SAME photo correctly exposed, while ImageHero's hero photo
(regardless of which corner-clipping technique it uses) looks brighter.
Since the one thing both ImageHero variants have in common (overflow-
hidden or not) is irrelevant to the actual difference, the real cause is
something else — see PROJECT-CONTEXT's Known Gaps for the updated, still-
open theory (possibly a perceptual/display effect rather than a code bug
at all — Marco separately noted it's "reactive to screen brightness").

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed (unchanged). `npm run build`
verified clean.

ROUND 141 — KCAL DECIMALS FIXED, PHOTO BRIGHTNESS EXPERIMENT (2026-07-04)
================================================================
Files: src/domain/{calc,calc.test}.ts, src/ui/screens/PantryScreen.tsx,
       src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx, src/ui/kit/ImageHero.tsx

── DECIMALS LEAKED BACK INTO KCAL / LIST-ROW PROTEIN ────────────────────────
Marco: Day's-log total kcal showing e.g. "50.2 kcal" instead of "50 kcal";
same decimal leak on Pantry list row protein and the Recent/search rows in
the add-food picker. Root cause: calc.ts's shared `round()` helper rounds
to 1 DECIMAL (matches the established macro-gram convention: protein/carbs/
fiber/fat allow 1 decimal), but round 138 used the SAME helper for calories
too in the new mealItemNutrition() and the pre-existing nutritionFor()/
sumSnapshots() — calories are supposed to always be a clean whole number.
Fixed: new `roundKcal()` (plain Math.round) used for calories specifically
in all three functions; `round()` stays 1-decimal for macros only. Also
fixed protein specifically in the Pantry list row and the Recent/search
picker rows to whole numbers (Math.round) — those are list/summary
contexts, not the 1-decimal detail view (MacroSummaryLine, untouched,
still correctly shows e.g. "Protein 5.6g"). One test updated (413 instead
of 412.5 — same math, now rounded to a whole kcal instead of a decimal).

── PHOTO "TOO BRIGHT" — EXPERIMENT, NOT A CONFIRMED FIX ─────────────────────
Marco clarified with a sharp diagnostic: the photo itself is fine — tap-
and-hold (iOS's native long-press preview) shows it correctly exposed, but
the app's normal on-screen rendering looks washed out. This rules out a
DATA/encoding problem (round 137's canvas colorSpace fix was addressing
the wrong layer) — the bytes are fine, since a raw-decode preview looks
right. Searched every food-photo render site + all of index.css: found
ZERO CSS filters, blend modes, overlays, or loading-state effects anywhere
in the codebase. Leading remaining theory: a WebKit/iOS compositing quirk
where an <img> clipped via a wrapping `overflow-hidden` + `border-radius`
+ `box-shadow` container gets promoted to its own GPU-composited layer,
which iOS's native long-press preview (a different, non-WebKit render
path) bypasses entirely — this would explain the exact symptom without
any app-code cause.
EXPERIMENT: in ImageHero.tsx (the Food item/Meal detail hero photo —
exactly what Marco's screenshot showed), moved `rounded-[20px]` from the
wrapping `overflow-hidden` div onto the `<img>` itself, removing the
`overflow-hidden` (an img can clip its own corners directly since it's a
replaced element — no need for a parent to do it). Visually identical
either way — this is a structural change only, not a guaranteed fix. Needs
Marco to test and report back whether it changes anything.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed (1 updated). `npm run build`
verified clean.

ROUND 140 — STEPPER INPUT: FIXED WIDTH + KILL THE FOCUS OUTLINE (2026-07-03)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx

Two follow-ups from round 139's tap-to-type on the Day's-log stepper,
both visible in Marco's screenshot (a stretched green-outlined bar with
iOS's selection-handle dots instead of the small pill):
- WIDTH CHANGE: the value box used `min-w-[54px]`, which is fine for a
  <span> (shrink-wraps to its text) but not for an <input> — inputs don't
  shrink-wrap and happily render much wider than a bare minimum. Switched
  to a fixed `w-[54px] shrink-0` so editing mode is pixel-identical to
  the static label.
- GREEN FOCUS OUTLINE: `outline-none`/`focus:outline-none` Tailwind
  classes were losing to the app's global `:focus-visible { outline: 2px
  solid var(--color-accent) }` rule (index.css) — same CSS specificity,
  and the global rule comes later in the stylesheet, so it won. Replaced
  with an inline `style={{ outline: 'none' }}`, which always wins
  regardless of specificity/source order.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed (unchanged). `npm run build`
verified clean.

ROUND 138 — MEAL NAME NOT STICKING + GRAMS-TO-SERVINGS RUNAWAY KCAL (2026-07-03)
================================================================
Files: src/domain/{types,calc}.ts(+test), src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx

── MEAL NAME EDITS NEVER STUCK ───────────────────────────────────────────────
Marco: built a local (not-saved-to-pantry) meal from "Describe" + "Photo",
meal saved under the FIRST item's name instead of the name field's value;
Day's-log row showed that first-item name; reopening showed the original
suggested title again; editing the name and re-saving did nothing.
Root cause: LogEntryContent.save()'s "stay a meal" branch correctly wrote
the edited name into mealData.name, but never touched entry.manualName —
and TodayScreen's row reads `entry.manualName ?? mealData?.name ?? ...`,
manualName first. Since manualName was frozen at whatever it was on the
very first save, the row (and the value carried into re-renders) never
reflected any later edit. Fixed: manualName is now kept in sync
(`saveToPantry ? undefined : mealName`) on every save, matching exactly how
logBasket already does it on first save.

── GRAMS SILENTLY BECAME "SERVINGS", ONE MEAL HIT 58,000 KCAL ───────────────
Marco: built a meal from two AI-scanned items (Describe + Photo — both
gram-based). After saving/reopening, the per-item stepper showed "servings"
instead of grams; a few taps of + sent the meal to 58,000 kcal.
Root cause: MealItem (the type behind FoodEntry.mealData.items) never
stored its measurementType/referenceAmount — only a flattened TOTAL
calories value plus an optional plain `qty` multiplier. entryToBasket()
(used every time an existing meal is reopened for editing) therefore
always hardcoded every item to `measurementType: 'per_serving',
referenceAmount: 1, qty: 1` — the "1 serving" here silently meant "the
entire scanned total." Tapping the stepper's +0.5 step then multiplied the
WHOLE total each time instead of nudging a few grams, which is exactly how
a two-item meal reaches five figures in a handful of taps.
Fixed (mirrors round 136's manualMeasurementType/manualReferenceAmount
pattern, applied to MealItem instead of FoodEntry):
- MealItem gets measurementType?/referenceAmount? fields. New meal items
  now store the RATE at referenceAmount + the real current qty (grams or
  servings) — same convention FoodItem/BasketItem already use — instead of
  a flattened total. Older items (no measurementType) are untouched and
  keep behaving exactly as before; no migration needed.
- New mealItemNutrition() (calc.ts) scales a MealItem correctly by its own
  measurementType factor; effectiveNutrition()'s meal branch and
  LogEntryContent.save()'s snapshot calc both now use it instead of a blind
  `calories * qty` multiply.
- entryToBasket() reconstructs each item with its real measurementType/
  referenceAmount when present, so the stepper offers the right increment
  (10g vs 0.5 servings) on reopen.
- Bonus fix found while in this code: entryToBasket()'s meal branch never
  carried a linked ingredient's pantryItemId back into the edit basket, so
  resaving a meal with a pantry-linked ingredient would silently unlink it
  (or duplicate the Food item, if "Save to pantry" was checked). Now
  restored via `pantryItemId: item.foodItemId`.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 48 passed (3 new). `npm run build` verified
clean.

================================================================
ROUND 137 — HDR PHOTO OVEREXPOSURE FIX (2026-07-03)
================================================================
Files: src/lib/image.ts

── PHOTOS SOMETIMES CAME OUT "EXTRA BRIGHT" AFTER SAVING ────────────────────
Marco noticed some photos he captured looked overexposed once saved to a
Food item, compared to how they actually looked. Traced to the two canvas-
based downscale helpers (downscaleImage for file/library picks,
downscaleDataUrl for Capacitor Camera captures used by Nutri-scan/Camera
scan flows) — both called getContext('2d') with no explicit color space.
iPhone's Smart HDR/Deep Fusion photos carry a wide-gamut/HDR profile, and
WebKit's canvas has a known history of mis-tone-mapping such sources when
the color space isn't pinned, blowing out highlights. Only affects photos
iOS actually shot in HDR (scene/lighting dependent), matching the
"sometimes" pattern. Fixed by passing { colorSpace: 'srgb' } explicitly to
both getContext('2d') calls, forcing WebKit to normalize the draw to the
display-safe range instead of mis-handling the source profile.

NOTE: this is a targeted fix for a documented WebKit canvas quirk, not a
guaranteed 100% cure — needs Marco to confirm on a photo he knows came out
too bright before. Separately found (not touched): PantryScreen/PhotoPicker
has a native-picker "Prompt" path that bypasses this canvas pipeline
entirely (stores the raw capture at quality:100) — if brightness shows up
from THAT specific button too, that would point to a different cause and is
worth knowing which exact icon Marco used.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 45 passed. `npm run build` verified clean.

================================================================
ROUND 136 — MANUAL ENTRY UNIT LOSS, MODAL SIZE, DECIMAL COMMA (2026-07-02)
================================================================
Files: src/domain/{types,calc}.ts(+test), src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx,
       src/ui/components/MethodCards.tsx, src/ui/kit/NumberField.tsx

── MANUAL/SCANNED SINGLE ENTRIES LOST THEIR UNIT ON REOPEN ──────────────────
Root cause of Marco's report ("saved as 100g, bumped to 400g, saved — but it
converted to servings"): a manually-logged entry (Nutri-scan, Describe,
Photo, or plain Manual — anything NOT picked from Pantry) only ever stored
its flattened TOTAL snapshot, never its measurementType/referenceAmount/
quantity. entryToBasket() had no way to know it was "400g" rather than "1
serving of the total" and always rebuilt it as the latter — silently
discarding whatever unit and quantity it actually was the moment you
reopened it.
Added manualMeasurementType/manualReferenceAmount to FoodEntry (quantity was
already there, just unused for manual entries). New unscaleSnapshot()
(calc.ts, tested) reverses nutritionFor()'s scaling — needed because the
stored snapshot is the TOTAL, and reconstructing a basket item needs the
PER-REFERENCE-AMOUNT values so re-scaling by quantity reproduces the same
total rather than doubling it. Wired into every place a manual entry gets
created or re-saved: logBasket's fresh single-item log, LogEntryContent's
"shrink back to manual" and "stay manual" save branches, and the
unlink-from-pantry conversion added in round 134. Older entries logged
before this round simply fall back to "1 serving" on reopen, same as
always — no migration needed, nothing breaks.

── METHODPICKERMODAL: TALLER + INSTRUCTIONAL LINE ───────────────────────────
Added a short caption ("Choose one way to add this food") between the title
and the method row, with extra padding — makes the modal noticeably taller
per Marco's ask, and tells the user what they're choosing between.

── NUMBERFIELD: DECIMAL COMMA REGRESSION FIXED ──────────────────────────────
Macro/quantity number fields were type="number", which — on locale keyboards
that produce "," for the decimal separator — silently REJECTS the comma
outright rather than accepting it for conversion (there was no comma-to-
period logic in the current code at all to have "broken"; type="number"
inputs just don't let non-canonical decimal characters through). Switched to
type="text" + inputMode="decimal" (still shows the same numeric keypad) with
explicit onChange normalization: "," -> ".", strip anything else that isn't
a digit or the first period.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 45 passed (+3 new unscaleSnapshot cases).
`npm run build` verified clean.

ROUND 135 — METHOD MODAL POLISH + UNLINK CHECKBOX FIXES (2026-07-02)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/MethodCards.tsx, src/ui/screens/PantryScreen.tsx,

── METHODPICKERMODAL NOW USES THE REAL SHEET COMPONENT ──────────────────────
Marco asked for: nicer open/close animation, an X on the left, drag-to-
dismiss, and the title changed from "Add food" to "New food". Realized the
existing Sheet component already does all four (slide animation, X-close on
the left, title centred, drag-to-dismiss with spring-back) — MethodPickerModal
was hand-rolling a raw createPortal div instead of just using it. Rewritten
as a thin wrapper: `<Sheet title="New food" onClose={onDismiss}>` (not
forceExpanded, so it auto-sizes to content like the old modal did). Far less
code, and inherits Sheet's already-proven behaviour instead of reinventing it.

Bug: the checkbox's own duplicate-name check started firing once round 134
made it interactive (previously it silently never ran, since the state
defaulted to false while the checkbox displayed checked+disabled — a
harmless mismatch until now) — but EditOverlay never passed existingItemId,
so the item's own pantry record matched itself as a "conflict." Fixed:
EditOverlay now passes existingItemId={item.pantryItemId}.
Copy: label now always reads "Save to pantry" (was "Saved to pantry" for
the already-linked case).
UX refinement (Marco's request): the checkbox now starts unchecked AND
disabled when editing an already-linked item — matching a "nothing to
decide until you've changed something" pattern. The moment any field
actually differs from what the item started with (new editedFromInitial
comparison — the existing `dirty` flag doesn't work here since basket-edit
mode starts pre-filled, not empty), it becomes enabled and shows as already
checked (the safe default: stays synced with Pantry, round 131), letting the
user explicitly uncheck it to unlink instead. Implemented as a purely
derived value (effectiveSaveToPantry), not a setState-inside-useEffect —
the project's lint rules disallow synchronous setState in effects, and the
derived approach is simpler anyway. Also guarded Save itself: for this case
specifically, Save is disabled until something's actually changed, so
tapping it on an untouched item can never be misread as "user chose to
unlink" (nothing changed = nothing to decide = button does nothing, rather
than silently unlinking).

ROUND 133 — CORRECTNESS PASS: NAMES, UNITS, DUPLICATES, COPY (2026-07-02)
================================================================
Files: src/domain/calc.ts(+test), src/domain/pantry.ts, src/data/quantityConversion.ts
       (NEW, +test), src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx, src/ui/screens/PantryScreen.tsx,
       src/ui/components/{FoodItemForm,PantryFoodItemDetail,PantryMealDetail,
       AddEntrySheet}.tsx

Marco's latest test pass surfaced several more real bugs plus a batch of
copy/UX requests. Correctness fixes first, then the smaller asks.

── "FOOD" PLACEHOLDER NAME FOR MEAL ENTRIES ─────────────────────────────────
TodayScreen's Day's-log row name used `entry.manualName ?? labelFor(items,
entry.foodItemId)`, where labelFor() falls back to the literal string 'Food'
if no pantry item matches. Meal entries (mealId set, foodItemId undefined)
always missed both checks and fell through to 'Food' — even though the row's
CALORIE total was already correct (effectiveNutrition already checks
mealData first, per round 123). Fixed: mealData.name is now checked before
the pantry-item fallback.

── QUANTITY/UNIT CONVERSION ON MEASUREMENTTYPE CHANGE (blocking, per Marco) ─
Root cause of "100g item, switch to per-serving, Day's log shows 100x
calories": a stored quantity (e.g. "100", meaning 100 grams under per_100g)
was being reinterpreted directly under the NEW unit (100 *servings*) with no
conversion — nutritionFor() has no way to know the number's old meaning.
New convertQuantity() (calc.ts) converts a quantity between unit bases,
preserving the actual physical amount. New convertFoodItemReferences()
(src/data/quantityConversion.ts) walks every FoodEntry (plain + Meal-linked
items) and every Meal referencing a Food item and converts their stored
quantity whenever that item's measurementType/referenceAmount changes.
Wired into every place that edits an existing Food item's unit: Pantry's
Food item detail, Meal detail's per-item edit, and the Day's-log basket's
edit-of-an-already-linked-item flow (both FoodForm and LogEntryContent).
Covered by dedicated tests (data/quantityConversion.test.ts) proving
nutrition is unchanged for the same physical amount after a unit switch.

── DUPLICATE NAMES ACROSS FOOD ITEMS AND MEALS ──────────────────────────────
findByName() only ever checked Food items against Food items — a Food item
and a Meal could share a name, confusing since the "All" list combines them.
New findPantryNameConflict() (domain/pantry.ts) checks a candidate name
against BOTH lists. Wired into every Food item create/edit surface
(FoodItemFormContent, now taking existingMeals) and into Meal renaming
(PantryMealDetail.saveName, which previously had no duplicate check at all —
reverts the input and toasts if a conflict is found).

── PICKING A MEAL VIA SEARCH NOW BEHAVES LIKE PICKING FROM RECENT ───────────
addPantryMeal's fast-path (populate the basket directly from the Meal,
prefill its name, default "Save to pantry" on, remember its id so re-saving
updates it) previously only triggered when NOT found via search — picking
the same Meal via search silently fell through to the "flatten in" path
instead, leaving the name field blank and the checkbox unchecked/generic.
Dropped the fromSearch condition for Meals — picking one into an empty
basket always means "log this meal," regardless of how it was found.

── RE-LOGGING AN ALREADY-LOGGED ITEM MERGES INSTEAD OF DUPLICATING ──────────
Fast-tapping a pantry item that's already logged today (as its own entry,
not part of a meal) now bumps that entry's quantity instead of creating a
second one — mirrors the basket's own "already there, increment" behaviour.
Undo restores the previous quantity rather than deleting the entry.

── COPY / CTA REQUESTS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
- meal-add-item mode ("Add item to meal") now shows the "Save to pantry"
  checkbox and its CTA reads "Add to meal" (was "Save & add to meal") —
  lets the user opt a new ingredient into visible Pantry status at creation
  time instead of always defaulting hidden.
- Editing a currently-hidden item's own record: CTA now reads "Save food
  item" when the checkbox is checked, "Update food item" when it isn't —
  previously always said "Update food item" regardless.
- Pantry Meal detail's Close button is now the primary CTA, labelled
  "Save meal".

ROUND 132 — CONVERT-TO-MEAL: STALE foodItemId HID EVERY OTHER ITEM (2026-07-02)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx

Root-caused Marco's "convert to meal, then save — I only see the first item
when I reopen it" (the Day's-log LIST ROW showed the correct combined
total, only reopening the entry was wrong — that split was the key clue).

entryToBasket() checked entry.foodItemId (and rebuilt a single-item basket
from it) BEFORE checking entry.mealData. Converting a plain Food entry into
a Meal never cleared its old foodItemId — save()'s meal branch only ever
SET mealId, it didn't unset the field the entry no longer needed. So the
entry ended up with both foodItemId (stale, from before) and mealData (new)
set at once. effectiveNutrition() already checks mealData first (round 123),
which is why the Day's-log row totaled correctly — but entryToBasket() did
not, so reopening the entry rebuilt the editor from the stale single-item
link and silently dropped every other item in the meal.
Fixed both ends: entryToBasket() now checks mealData first, matching
effectiveNutrition()'s existing order; and save()'s meal branch now
explicitly clears foodItemId when converting an entry to a Meal, so there's
no stale field left for anything to trip over next time either.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 35 passed. `npm run build` verified clean.

Nutri-scan follow-up (not a code change): the scanned result Marco saw
(measurementType per_100g, qty = the estimated grams, no serving-choice
prompt) matches exactly how Camera/Photo scanning works by design — no
prompt, always per-100g, grams estimated directly. The dedicated Nutri-scan
(label) button DOES prompt for per-100g vs per-serving via ServingModal.
Asked Marco to confirm which icon he actually tapped before treating this
as a bug.

ROUND 131 — DAY'S-LOG EDIT FLOW: LIVE-LINK BUGS (2026-07-02)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx

Marco's testing surfaced several real, pre-existing bugs in the Day's-log
basket edit flow (not introduced by the meals-in-pantry work, but found
while stress-testing it) — all in how editing a basket item talks to the
Pantry.

── ROOT CAUSE: "SAVE TO PANTRY" GATED A WRITE THAT SHOULDN'T HAVE BEEN GATED ─
FoodItemFormContent only returns saveToPantry: true when its checkbox is
actually shown, and the checkbox is hidden once an item is ALREADY
pantry-linked (shown as "Saved to pantry", checked+disabled, instead). Both
EditOverlay onSave handlers (FoodForm's fresh-log basket AND
LogEntryContent's edit-existing-entry basket) used that boolean to decide
whether to write to repos.foodItems at all — so editing an ALREADY-linked
item's macros updated the in-memory basket row (looked fine in the moment)
but never reached the actual Food item record. Reopen the entry, or view it
anywhere else that reads live, and the edit was gone.
Fixed by decoupling "is this item pantry-linked" from "did the user just
check a box": both onSave handlers now write through to repos.foodItems
whenever the item IS or BECOMES linked, only using the checkbox to decide
whether a brand-new item gets linked in the first place.

── A SECOND BUG THIS SAME GATE WAS HIDING ───────────────────────────────────
LogEntryContent's version of this handler, when a manual entry got newly
linked to a pantry item, set entry.foodItemId but never entry.quantity.
effectiveNutrition() requires BOTH to be present to live-recompute — with
quantity missing, the entry looked linked but stayed frozen on its original
snapshot forever. This is exactly Marco's "Wednesday's entry never updated"
report: today's entry (added via search, which sets quantity correctly)
updated fine; Wednesday's entry (retrofitted via edit → save to pantry,
missing quantity) never did. Fixed as part of the same rewrite — quantity
and a fresh snapshot are now always included.

── THIRD: OUTER "SAVE CHANGES" USED A STALE REFERENCE ───────────────────────
LogEntryContent.save()'s single-item branch compared basket[0].pantryItemId
against the `pantryItem` prop — resolved ONCE from entry.foodItemId at
mount. A link created mid-session (via the edit overlay) wouldn't match that
stale prop, risking the save falling through to the manual-entry branch and
losing the new link. Now checks basket[0].pantryItemId directly.

── FOURTH: SWITCHING UNITS DIDN'T RESET QUANTITY ────────────────────────────
Editing a basket item's measurementType (per_100g ↔ per_serving) kept the
old raw qty number as-is — a value that meant "grams" suddenly meant
"servings" (or vice versa) with no conversion, producing wildly wrong totals
and a stepper stuck on odd values (Marco's screenshot: 8.9g became "8.9
srv"). Now resets qty to a sensible default (100 / 1) whenever
measurementType actually changes during an edit.

── FIFTH: LOGENTRYSHEET TITLE ────────────────────────────────────────────────
Per Marco's original reference screenshot, tapping an existing Day's-log
entry should show a generic "Food item" / "Meal" title (matching Pantry's
own detail screens), not the entry's actual name. LogEntrySheet's title is
now derived state, reported up live from LogEntryContent's basket size via
onTitleChange — converting a Food entry into a Meal mid-edit flips the
header immediately, before Save is even tapped.

── CLEANUP ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
4 stray `eslint-disable react-hooks/refs` comments (pre-existing, unrelated
to this round's changes but surfaced once nearby code shifted) removed —
ESLint confirmed they weren't suppressing anything.

tsc: clean. lint: clean (0 warnings). vitest: 35 passed (unchanged).
`npm run build` verified clean.

Still open, needs more specific repro from Marco (see chat): "convert an
existing Food entry to a Meal, then Save — not working" wasn't reproduced by
code review alone; and the Nutri-scan flow not showing the per-100g/per-
serving prompt on this particular scan.

ROUND 130 — MEALS IN PANTRY: NEW INGREDIENTS DON'T POLLUTE THE PANTRY (2026-07-02)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/screens/PantryScreen.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx,
       src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx, src/ui/components/PantryPicker.tsx,
       src/ui/components/FoodItemForm.tsx, src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx,
       src/data/mealsMigration.ts, src/data/mealsMigration.test.ts

Marco's call after testing: Food items created purely to complete a meal in
the Pantry meal-builder shouldn't show up in the main Pantry list by
default. They "live only in the meal" — visible/editable from inside it, but
not cluttering the Food-items tab — unless the user explicitly opts one in
from its own edit view.

── NEW MEAL-ONLY INGREDIENTS DEFAULT TO isArchived: true ────────────────────
PantryFoodItemDetail.handleAddMealItem and PantryMealDetail.handleAddItem
(both create a brand-new Food item via Manual) now create it with
isArchived: true instead of false. Reused the existing isArchived flag
rather than adding a new field — it already does exactly the right thing at
the repo layer (foodItems.all() excludes it by default, foodItems.all(true)
includes it), and round 123's migration already established the precedent
of "archived but still resolvable" for exactly this kind of hidden-but-real
item. mealsMigration.ts's recovered ingredient items get the same treatment
now — legacy meals won't suddenly dump a pile of previously-invisible
ingredients into Marco's real Pantry list once this ships.
Explicitly NOT affected: the Meal itself (always visible when created), and
any Food item picked via "Add from pantry" (it was already a real,
consciously-saved pantry item before being added to this meal).

── RIPPLE FIX: MEAL CALCULATIONS MUST RESOLVE HIDDEN INGREDIENTS TOO ────────
Hiding these items from `foodItems.all()` means every place that builds an
itemsById map to compute a Meal's live nutrition/photo (mealNutritionFor,
mealPhotoFor, mealPhotosFor) would otherwise silently treat a hidden
ingredient as "missing" and contribute 0 — breaking the very meal it belongs
to. Fixed everywhere a Meal's own ingredients get resolved, by threading an
additional `allItems` (foodItems.all(true)) alongside the existing
visible-only `items`:
  - PantryScreen: fetches the full set once, filters visible for its own
    Food-items list, builds itemsById from the full set for Meal rows.
  - PantryMealDetail / PantryFoodItemDetail: take a new `allItems` prop,
    used for resolving the current Meal's items and passed through to
    PantryPicker (whose own listed Meal rows have the same problem).
  - PantryPicker / Day's-log FoodPicker: new optional `allItems` prop,
    defaults to `items`, used only for itemsById.
  - Day's-log addPantryMeal (both FoodForm and LogEntryContent): now
    resolves a picked Meal's ingredients from the full set — previously a
    hidden ingredient would have been silently DROPPED when logging a saved
    meal that contained one.
Everywhere else (duplicate-name checks, "New food" creation, what's offered
as pickable in Add-from-pantry) still correctly uses the visible-only set —
hidden items shouldn't appear as legitimate standalone pantry entries.

── OPT-IN: "SAVE TO PANTRY" ON A HIDDEN ITEM'S OWN EDIT VIEW ────────────────
FoodItemFormContent's pantry-edit mode now shows the "Save to pantry"
checkbox when editing a currently-hidden item (initial.isArchived === true).
Checking it and saving flips isArchived → false (visible → hidden is not
offered here — that's what deleting the item is for). Wired in
PantryMealDetail's confirmUpdate; toast reads "Saved to pantry" instead of
the generic "Food item updated" when this happens. (Not wired in
PantryFoodItemDetail — items reached through that screen are always already
visible, since hidden ones never appear in the list you'd tap into it from.)

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 35 passed (unchanged count; mealsMigration
test cases extended to assert the new isArchived default). `npm run build`
verified clean.

  the basket cards in both FoodForm (fresh log) and LogEntryContent (editing

items only, "per serving"/"per 100g" subtitle) — confirmed against the same
mockup reference, which shows this list unchanged from its current form; no
Meals-picking added there. Sheet title also stays static "Add" throughout
(not a dynamic "Food item"/"Meal" title) — the mockup confirms this too.

ROUND 127 — MEALS IN PANTRY: DAY'S-LOG INTEGRATION (2026-07-02)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx

The piece deferred since round 123: logging/saving a Meal from the Day's-log
now creates and maintains a REAL linked Meal, instead of the old behaviour of
collapsing a multi-item basket into one Food item with summed macros.

── LOGBASKET (FRESH LOG) ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
When basket.length > 1: each basket item is resolved to a real Food item id
— items already picked from Pantry (pantryItemId set) keep that link
REGARDLESS of the "Save to pantry" checkbox (previously this link was always
dropped the moment items were grouped into a meal); brand-new/manual/scanned
items only get a real Food item created for them when "Save to pantry" is
checked (spec §4 — unsaved items inside a saved Meal get saved internally
too; we don't silently pollute the Pantry when the user didn't ask to save).
When "Save to pantry" is checked, a real Meal (with MealFoodItem entries
pointing at those resolved Food items) is created and the FoodEntry gets
mealId set. mealData.items now always carries foodItemId per item, so
effectiveNutrition() can live-recompute — even for a meal that ISN'T saved to
Pantry, any of its items that came from Pantry still update live if you edit
that Food item later. The Meal's photo defaults to the first captured photo
from the log flow.

── LOGENTRYCONTENT (EXISTING ENTRY → CONVERT TO / EDIT A MEAL) ──────────────
This screen never had a "Save to pantry" option before — spec §8's flow
("existing Food entry → Change to Meal → optionally Save to pantry → Save
changes") wasn't actually wired up. Added: checkbox (pre-checked when the
entry is already linked to a real Meal), same real-Meal resolution logic as
logBasket, and correct create-vs-update behaviour — re-saving an
already-linked entry updates the SAME Meal (by id) rather than spawning a
duplicate every time you edit. Unchecking the box un-links the entry
(mealId cleared) without deleting the Meal object itself from Pantry.
CTA renamed "Save" → "Save changes" per spec copy.
Bugfix while in here: shrinking a meal entry back down to a single item (by
removing basket rows) now explicitly clears mealId/mealData — previously
these were left stale on the entry (pre-existing issue, not introduced this
round, but directly relevant to not leaving a dangling mealId around).

── NOT COVERED THIS ROUND ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
Camera/Photo/Describe for the PANTRY meal-builder (still "coming soon" —
needs those helpers extracted from this same file, unrelated to today's
change). No new automated test coverage for this logic specifically — it's
procedural DB-writing code inside a large UI component (AddEntrySheet.tsx),
not domain logic, so it doesn't fit the existing "pure functions in
calc.ts get vitest coverage" pattern; the app.preview.test.tsx suite that
COULD exercise this end-to-end is currently skipped in this sandbox (pre-
existing, unrelated to this change). This is exactly the kind of change
where a real on-device pass matters most.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 35 passed (8 pre-existing skips, unchanged
by this round). `npm run build` verified clean.

ROUND 123 — MEALS IN PANTRY: FOUNDATION (DATA MODEL, MIGRATION, PANTRY UI) (2026-07-02)
================================================================
Files: src/domain/types.ts, src/domain/calc.ts, src/data/repositories.ts,
       src/data/db.ts, src/data/dexieRepositories.ts, src/data/memoryRepositories.ts,
       src/data/mealsMigration.ts (NEW), src/data/mealsMigration.test.ts (NEW),
       src/domain/calc.test.ts, src/state/repos.ts, src/ui/AppShell.tsx,
       src/ui/kit/MacroSummaryLine.tsx (NEW), src/ui/kit/index.ts,
       src/ui/components/PantryItemCard.tsx (NEW),
       src/ui/components/PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx (NEW),
       src/ui/components/PantryMealDetail.tsx (NEW),
       src/ui/components/FoodItemForm.tsx, src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/PantryScreen.tsx

Kickoff of the "meals in pantry" work — a longer, multi-round project scoped
from Marco's taxonomy doc + detail spec doc + Figma flow exports (Food & meals/
folder). This round lays the Pantry-side foundation; Day's-log integration
(logging a Meal, converting an existing Food entry into a Meal, camera/photo/
describe methods in the meal builder) is intentionally deferred to a follow-up
round rather than risking a rushed change to the daily-use logging flow.

── DOMAIN MODEL: MEAL AS A REAL PANTRY ENTITY ───────────────────────────────
Previously "meal" only existed as FoodEntry.mealData — a display-only snapshot
attached to ONE Day's-log entry, never a reusable Pantry object. Added:
  - Meal { id, name, photo?, items: MealFoodItem[], isArchived } — new type.
  - MealFoodItem { id, foodItemId, quantity } — one ingredient's amount inside
    a Meal, same unit-basis convention as FoodEntry.quantity.
  - MealItem gains foodItemId? — lets a Day's-log Meal item link back to its
    Pantry Food item (previous mealData items were always frozen snapshots
    with no link back, even when originally picked from the Pantry).
  - FoodEntry gains mealId? — linked/local distinction for Meal entries,
    mirroring foodItemId's role for plain Food entries.
A Meal never stores its own macros: calc.ts's new mealNutritionFor(meal,
itemsById) always sums live from the current Food items, so editing an
ingredient's macros anywhere instantly updates every Meal (and, once an item
carries foodItemId, every Meal entry) that uses it — same philosophy as
effectiveNutrition() already applied to plain Food entries.
effectiveNutrition() extended: Meal entries (mealData present) now recompute
per-item — items with foodItemId pull live from the current pantry item;
items without one (local/unlinked, or the pantry item was later deleted) fall
back to their own stored macro values. Existing local meal entries are
numerically unaffected (verified via new calc.test.ts cases): they simply sum
the same stored per-item values that used to sit frozen in entry.snapshot.

── REPOSITORIES / DB ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
MealRepo interface (all/put/remove) added to Repositories + ExportBundle.
Dexie schema bumped to v2, adding a `meals` table and a `mealId` index on
foodEntries. memoryRepositories gets the same shape for the preview build.

── LEGACY DATA MIGRATION (src/data/mealsMigration.ts, NEW) ──────────────────
Before Meals existed, logging 2+ basket items together and ticking "Save to
pantry" collapsed the whole group into a single per-serving Food item holding
the SUMMED macros (see round ≤122's logBasket). One-time migration, gated by
a localStorage flag, run once from AppShell's mount effect (real/Dexie store
only — the preview build boots a fresh store every load, nothing to migrate):
  - Finds Food items shaped like a collapsed meal (per_serving, referenceAmount
    1) that are referenced by a FoodEntry carrying mealData with >=2 items.
  - Creates one real Food item per recovered ingredient (dedup by name against
    existing pantry items), combines them into a new Meal, re-points every
    owning entry at the Meal (mealId set, per-item foodItemId stamped), and
    ARCHIVES (never deletes) the old collapsed Food item so nothing referencing
    it by id can break.
  - Conservative by design: if fewer than 2 ingredients can be recovered from
    mealData, the Food item is left exactly as it was.
Covered by src/data/mealsMigration.test.ts against memoryRepositories
(migration function takes an injectable Repositories, defaulting to the real
app repos, specifically so it's unit-testable without a live IndexedDB).

── PANTRY UI ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
PantryScreen: the All / per 100g / per serving filter pills are replaced with
Food items / Meals — the meaningful split now is what KIND of reusable object
something is, not its serving-unit setup (per Marco's call — simpler than a
separate Items/Meals tab). Meal rows show live totals via mealNutritionFor().
Tapping a Food item no longer jumps straight to the edit form — it now opens
a new Food item detail sheet (PantryFoodItemDetail.tsx): photo, name/kcal,
macro line, a permanently-disabled serving stepper (Pantry defines properties,
never consumed quantity — spec §10), edit pencil, top-right delete, and a
"Create a meal" module. The Manual method is fully wired (opens "Add item to
meal", creates the second Food item, creates the Meal, hands off to Meal
detail); Camera/Photo/Describe are visually present but show a "coming soon"
toast — wiring them up reuses scan/describe infra that currently lives
un-exported inside AddEntrySheet.tsx, left for a follow-up round.
New PantryMealDetail.tsx: editable meal name, one card per Food item (via the
new shared PantryItemCard), "+ Add a new food item", card-level remove (hidden
when only one item remains — a Meal can never have zero items), and a
top-right delete for the whole Meal. Removing an item / deleting a Meal both
show an undo toast; editing an item's Food item goes through the same
confirm-before-update flow as Food item detail.

── EDIT-CONFIRMATION ON PANTRY FOOD ITEM UPDATES ────────────────────────────
Editing an existing Food item (from either Food item detail or a Meal's
per-item edit) now shows a sheet-on-sheet confirmation — "This will update all
records using this food item." / Update food item / Cancel — before the write
actually happens, per the spec's disclaimer copy. FoodItemFormContent's own
CTA copy updated to match the spec's copy tables: pantry-new "Save food"
(was "Save to pantry"), pantry-edit "Update food item" (was "Save changes").
Added mode 'meal-add-item' ("Save & add to meal" / loading "Saving & adding to
meal") for the new-item-inside-a-meal form. onSave may now return a Promise;
the form shows its own saving/loading label while awaiting it.

── SHARED COMPONENTS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
MacroSummaryLine (new kit atom): the "Protein 3.5g · Carbs 30g · Fibre 1g ·
Fat 6g" pill, one formatting rule shared by Day's-log basket cards AND the new
Pantry cards. BasketCard (AddEntrySheet.tsx, used by both the fresh-log basket
and LogEntrySheet's edit-existing-entry basket) now shows this macro line
between the name/kcal row and the stepper row, so a card's full nutrition is
visible without opening its editor (Marco's screenshot ask, this round).
PantryItemCard (new): the Pantry-side "Food item card" (spec §9). Deliberately
NOT a forced unification with Day's-log's BasketCard — the two surfaces have
opposite stepper rules (Pantry always disabled; Day's log always enabled) and
different trash semantics (remove-from-meal vs delete-whole-object vs
remove-from-basket). They share macro-line formatting and Icon affordances;
a fuller "one basket-view component, many touch points" unification (Marco's
point re: FoodItemForm's own multi-surface pattern) is a good candidate for a
dedicated follow-up round rather than a side-effect of this one, given
BasketCard is Marco's live daily-use surface with no device in the loop here.

── KNOWN GAPS AFTER THIS ROUND (see PROJECT-CONTEXT.txt) ────────────────────
Day's-log integration: logBasket's "Save to pantry" for a multi-item basket
still collapses into one Food item rather than creating a real linked Meal;
"Save changes" vs "Log meal" CTA + existing-entry-to-Meal conversion need
wiring to mealId once this lands. Meal nutrition summary (dedicated macro
breakdown, not just per-item cards) — noted as a TODO, not built. Camera/
Photo/Describe methods in the Pantry meal builder — deferred.

tsc: clean. lint: clean. vitest: 30 passed, 8 skipped (pre-existing skips,
unrelated to this round). `npm run build` (vite) also verified clean in the
Cowork sandbox this round, despite the earlier ENV NOTE about rollup — still
run the full `npm install && npm test && npm run build && npm run ios:sync`
verification on your Mac before trusting this on-device, per usual.

2. FoodForm call site: creates pantryId = newId() first; calls

ROUND 119 — MULTI-PHOTO COLLAGE, MEAL NAME FIELD, CHANGE BUTTON SCOPING (2026-06-30)
================================================================
Files: src/domain/types.ts, src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx
Commits: d76f30e, 31c818c

── TYPES: mealData.photos[] ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
Added `photos?: string[]` to FoodEntry.mealData alongside the existing
`photo?: string`. Backward-compatible: old entries with only a `photo` string
continue to work; new saves write both. Max 3 photos (ImageHero collage limit).

── FOODFORM: SAVE ALL SCAN PHOTOS + FIX MEAL NAME FALLBACK ─────────────────
logBasket now writes `photos: sourcePhotos.slice(0, 3)` into mealData on every
multi-item save. Previously only `photo: primaryPhoto` (first photo) was saved.
Fallback meal name changed from hard-coded `'Meal'` to `timeMealName()` which
returns "Breakfast" / "Lunch" / "Dinner" / "Snack" based on hour of day.
Previously, if the user left the meal name field blank, every logged meal was
saved as "Meal" and appeared that way in the Day's log.

── LOGENTRYCONTENT: MULTI-PHOTO INITIALISATION ──────────────────────────────
localPhotos state initialises via priority chain:
  1. entry.mealData.photos[] (new saved array)
  2. entry.mealData.photo (legacy single string)
  3. pantryItem.photo (pantry-linked entries with a photo)
  4. [] (no photo)
Scan photos added in the current session are appended and capped at 3.
On save, writes both photo (first) and photos[] to mealData.

── LOGENTRYCONTENT: EDITABLE MEAL NAME FIELD ────────────────────────────────
New `localMealName` state, initialised from `entry.mealData?.name ?? ''`.
When basket.length >= 2 and !editMode, a LabeledInput "Meal name" is shown
above the basket cards with placeholder=timeMealName() — identical position
and style to FoodForm's existing meal name field.
hasChanges includes localMealName change detection.
save() uses `localMealName.trim() || timeMealName()` so the name is always
meaningful, never blank.

── LOGENTRYCONTENT: CHANGE BUTTON SCOPED TO SCAN ITEMS ─────────────────────
onCorrect condition changed from `!item.pantryItemId` to `item.sourceId`.
Previously: every non-pantry item (including pre-existing meal items loaded
from storage) showed the "Change" button — wrong.
Now: only items freshly scanned in the current editing session carry a
sourceId (set by scanResultToBasket). Pre-existing meal items, manually-added
items, and pantry items all have no sourceId → no Change button.
correctingIdx state added; handleDescribeAnalyze splices the corrected items
in at the right index (same as FoodForm's fix flow).

tsc: clean. lint: clean.

ROUND 118 — LOGENTRYSHEET OVERHAUL + SERVINGMODAL + QUICK FIXES (2026-06-30)

Files: src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx, src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx
Commits: f0ea0f6 (main batch), c40aaa3 (context fix + photo + spacing)

── BASKETCARD CHANGE BUTTON: NO BORDER ─────────────────────────────────────
Removed `border border-border-strong` from Change button. Now styled with
`shadow-sm` only (no border). Cleaner appearance requested by Marco.

── ADDANOTHERSECTION: OVERFLOW-HIDDEN ───────────────────────────────────────
Added `overflow-hidden` to the outer `rounded-[24px] bg-surface-sunken` div.
CSS equivalent of Figma "Crop" — clips card shadow bleed at the container
boundary. The `-mb-3` bleed from MethodCards is now cleanly cropped.

── SERVINGMODAL: CREATEPORTAL + GRAB HANDLE + SAFE AREA ────────────────────
Root cause: ServingModal was a `fixed` div inside the Sheet's portal DOM tree,
creating a nested stacking context that prevented it from layering above
FloatingTabBar. Fix: wrapped in `createPortal(content, document.body)` so it
renders directly in body's stacking context (z-[300]).
Grab handle pill (`h-1.5 w-11 rounded-pill bg-border-strong`) added at top of
modal, same as Sheet.

for iPhone without bottom bar).

── LOGENTRYSHEET: SPLIT INTO SHELL + CONTENT ───────────────────────────────
Root cause of broken tap-to-edit and overlay hooks: useSheetSetOverlay and
useSheetSetOverlayBack were called in LogEntrySheet which renders <Sheet> as
its root. These hooks use useContext(SheetOverlaySetContext) which Sheet
provides to its *children*, not to the component that renders it → null context.

Fix: LogEntrySheet split into two components:
  LogEntrySheet — thin shell; computes title + pantryItem; creates delRef;
                  renders <Sheet> with trash button; passes LogEntryContent
                  as a child.
  LogEntryContent — lives inside Sheet's context tree; all state + overlay
                    hooks work correctly. Registers del() on delRef so the
                    trash button in the Sheet header can call it.

── LOGENTRYCONTENT: BUG FIXES ───────────────────────────────────────────────
hasChanges bug: entryToBasket was called TWICE (once for useState, once for
useRef) generating different newId() values → JSON strings never matched →
Save always visible. Fix: useState initializer writes to initialBasketRef.current
in the same call, uses eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/refs. IDs are
stripped (destructured away) before JSON comparison.

Section header: changed from conditional (meal/pantry/manual name) to always
"Meal" — consistent with FoodForm basket header.

Stepper delete last item: onRemove now checks `basket.length === 1` and calls
`del()` (deletes the whole FoodEntry + undo toast) instead of just filtering
the basket array to empty.

Inline add-another: replaced old button that called `onClose(); onAddMore()`
with a real AddAnotherSection + FoodPicker (camera, photo, describe, label,
manual) — same as FoodForm. onAddMore prop removed from LogEntrySheet and
TodayScreen call site.

Photos: localPhotos[] state tracks all photos in session. Initialised from
entry.mealData?.photo and pantryItem?.photo. Scan photos added via runScan
append to localPhotos (capped at 3). ImageHero renders multi-photo collage.

Save button: wrapped in `<div className="pt-3">` so gap = space-y-3 (12px)
+ pt-3 (12px) = 24px total above Save.

── TODAYSCREEN ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
onAddMore prop removed from LogEntrySheet call site.

ROUND 114 — DESCRIBE REDESIGN + SCAN FIXES + SHEET SCROLL + NAV CENTERING (2026-06-30)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx, src/ui/kit/Sheet.tsx,
       api/describe-food.ts (Vercel serverless)

── VERCEL DESCRIBE-FOOD ENDPOINT ────────────────────────────────────────────
Model changed from `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001` (unrecognised by deployed SDK
version, caused "Load failed" network errors) to `claude-sonnet-4-6`.
Prompt overhauled: enforces ≤22 char item names, rejects non-food images
(returns { foods: [] }), clearer JSON shape instruction, improved multi-item
detection.

── SHEET SCROLL FIX ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
`overflowX: 'hidden'` removed from both scroll containers in Sheet.tsx:
· OverlayLayer flex column outer div
· Sheet main scroll area
On iOS WKWebView, adding overflow-x hidden to a scroll container causes the
parent sheet to participate in lateral scroll chaining, making the whole sheet
slide side-to-side. Removing it fixes the movement without any visual change.

── OVERLAYNAVBAR CENTERING ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Back button wrapped in `<span className="w-10 shrink-0 flex items-center">`.
Previously the button's -m-3 negative margin was shifting the flex layout,
pulling the centred title off-axis. The w-10 span acts as a fixed-width anchor
matching the right placeholder span.

── ADD-ANOTHER SECTION: SINGLE CONTAINER ────────────────────────────────────
FoodPicker gained `bare?: boolean` prop. When bare=true, the component skips
its outer `rounded-[24px] bg-surface-sunken p-3` wrapper, so the picker
content expands inside AddAnotherSection's own surface without creating a
double-box. AddAnotherSection passes bare to FoodPicker. Orphan closing div
from the removed wrapper cleaned up.

ROUND 111 — ADD-ENTRY UX POLISH BATCH 2 (2026-06-29)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/kit/Sheet.tsx, src/ui/kit/index.ts, src/ui/kit/ImageHero.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/TodayScreen.tsx, src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx,
       src/ui/screens/PantryScreen.tsx

── OVERLAY SWIPE-TO-DISMISS + STICKY NAV ─────────────────────────────────────
Sheet.tsx received a set of overlay improvements that apply to every panel
app-wide:

· OverlayLayer now accepts an `onBack` prop and installs touch handlers.
  Swipe right ≥60px horizontal (with less than 60% vertical drift) calls onBack.
· OverlayScrolledContext (internal) tracks whether the overlay's scroll area
  has been scrolled. Provided to children via React context.
· OverlayNav (new export): a sticky `top-0` title bar rendered inside the
  overlay scroll container. Shows a subtle `border-bottom` shadow (via
  box-shadow) when scrolled. Back arrow left, centred title, optional right
  slot. All overlay components now use this instead of hand-rolled nav rows.
  Applies to: DescribeOverlay, LabelOverlay, ManualOverlay, EditOverlay,
  SheetInfoPanel (TodayScreen).
· useSheetSetOverlayBack (new export): hook that registers a back/dismiss
  function from inside a Sheet child. Sheet stores it as childOverlayBack
  and passes it to OverlayLayer. Registered in FoodForm (via overlayBackRef
  pattern) and InfoOverlaySlot (TodayScreen) so swipe works everywhere.
· SheetOverlayBackSetContext + childOverlayBack state + setChildOverlayBackCb
  added to Sheet. Provider wraps children alongside existing overlay/footer
  providers.

── IMAGE HERO ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Single photo changed from `aspect-[4/3] w-full` to `h-64 w-64` centred square
(256×256px). Multi-photo collage sizing deferred to a future round.

── TODAY SCREEN ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
· Frequent-foods quick-log grid (4-item grid at top of Diary section) removed.
  Related: quickLog() helper removed, freqIds/frequentFoods live queries
  removed, FREQUENT_MIN_LOGS/FREQUENT_MIN_FOODS constants removed, QuickLogCard
  + Thumb imports removed, frequentFoods prop removed from DayPanel.
· InfoOverlaySlot: added useSheetSetOverlayBack(onBack) so info panels in
  MacroDetailSheet and BreakdownSheet support swipe-to-go-back.
· SheetInfoPanel: replaced hand-rolled nav row with shared <OverlayNav>.

── ADD-ENTRY SHEET ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Log button: removed disabled={basket.length === 0} — always tappable.
Meal header: now hidden when basket is empty (was always visible).

addPantryItem: checks if same pantryItemId already in basket; if so,
increments qty by one step rather than creating a duplicate entry.

Swipe support: overlayBackRef pattern — overlayBack() assigns to ref during
render; useSheetSetOverlayBack(() => overlayBackRef.current()) registers it
once (stable effect deps).

BasketCard redesign:
· Removed photo thumbnail and protein label.
· Stepper always visible (was hidden in edit mode).
· Normal mode: tapping card anywhere → opens EditOverlay (calls onEdit).
  card has cursor:pointer + onClick handler.
· Card tracks local `swiped` state. Swipe left ≥50px (less vertical drift)
  → swiped=true → `revealed` flag. Swipe right ≥30px when swiped → reset.
  Tap when swiped → reset (dismiss).
· revealed = editMode || swiped. When revealed: card slides -translate-x-1
  (transition-transform 200ms) and a trash circle button appears to the right.
· isEditing prop: when this card's edit overlay closes (true→false), useEffect
  resets swiped to false (ESLint disable comment for set-state-in-effect).
· Trash button has stopPropagation to avoid triggering card's onClick.

BasketStepper: step delta can bring qty to 0. At 0 or below calls onRemove()
immediately (no negative qty). Shows a trash icon (text-danger) instead of
the qty label when qty ≤ 0. Outer div has onClick stopPropagation so stepper
taps don't bubble to the card's onEdit handler.

"Add another item" button: bg-surface-sunken + text-content (was bg-surface
+ text-accent shadow-card). Removed !editMode guard — visible in edit mode.
FoodPicker rendered whenever showPicker=true (removed !editMode guard too).

FoodPicker: new heading + onClose optional props. When heading is set, a row
appears at the top: X button (left) + centred title. Used when basket has
items and picker is expanded ("Add another item" expanded state). Recent items
"+" button changed from bg-accent/10 text-accent to bg-accent text-black.

MethodCards: text-content (was text-content-secondary). Overflow fix: outer
wrapper changed to -mx-3 -mb-3 overflow-x-auto with inner flex row using
px-3 pt-1 pb-3. This breaks the cards out of the parent's p-3 padding so
card shadows are not clipped by the container's border-radius on WKWebView.

ManualOverlay: added full-width photo upload (file input ref, FileReader,
localPhoto state). Photo section identical to EditOverlay: Add photo button
→ full-width image with trash (top-right) + "Change photo" (white-border
bottom-centre). onAdd signature extended with optional `photo?: string` field.
addManualItem updated to create a SourceGroup when photo is present, and to
pass photo to pantry item when saveToPantry is true.

EditOverlay:
· useOverlaySetFooter removed — no sticky footer.
· Nav row replaced with <OverlayNav title="Edit" onBack={onBack} /> (sticky,
  shadow on scroll, no right Cancel).
· Non-sticky Save button + Cancel text button added at bottom of scroll area.
· Photo section: trash icon (top-right, rounded-full bg-black/50) removes
  photo; "Change photo" is a rounded-full white-border button (text-[16px])

ROUND 110 — FAB MORPH ANIMATION + PICKER UI FIXES (2026-06-28)
================================================================
Files: src/ui/kit/FloatingTabBar.tsx, src/ui/components/AddEntrySheet.tsx,
       src/ui/components/TabBar.tsx, src/ui/AppShell.tsx,
       src/ui/kit/Sheet.tsx, src/ui/kit/SegmentedControl.tsx

── FAB MORPH ANIMATION ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FloatingTabBar rewritten with 7-phase clip-path morph animation. FAB tap no
longer opens the speed-dial; instead it kicks off a ~580ms forward sequence
and the sheet × button (or Log it) triggers the ~580ms reverse.

Phases:
  fwd1 (230ms)   FAB icon rotates 45° (×). Nav tab icons fade out (opacity 0).
                 White overlay fades in at pill clip-path position.
  fwd2 (350ms)   FAB hides. White overlay grows pill → full sheet via CSS
                 clip-path: inset(). Green CTA pill grows from 48px circle
                 to full-width 56px pill, centre anchored at FAB position via
                 transform: translate(-50%, 50%) + bottom: FAB_CENTER_BOTTOM.
  open           Morph overlay visually behind the Sheet portal (z-200).
                 Nav tab icons fade back in.
  rev-init       Snap overlay to full-screen (no transition) — called by
                 AppShell when sheet × is pressed.
  rev2 (350ms)   Overlay shrinks full → pill. CTA pill shrinks to 48px circle.
  rev1 (230ms)   Overlay fades out. Nav icons already visible. FAB fades back in.
  idle           Normal state.

CSS technique: clip-path: inset() transitions between:
  Pill: inset(calc(100% - max(0.75rem, env(safe-area-inset-bottom)) - 68px)
              1rem 0 1rem round 9999px)
  Full: inset(0 0 0 0 round 1.5rem 1.5rem 0 0)   ← matches Sheet top radius

Speed-dial code preserved (block-commented openMenu). ACTION_ITEMS array and
all button render logic intact for future re-enable.

New props on FloatingTabBar:
  onFabMorphComplete?: () => void  — called when fwd morph ends (→ open sheet)
  startFabReverseRef?: MutableRefObject<(() => void) | null>
    — FloatingTabBar populates this ref; AppShell calls it to trigger reverse

── APPSHELL COORDINATION ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
- startFabReverseRef: useRef<(() => void) | null>(null) — passed to TabBar/FloatingTabBar
- fabMorphOpenedSheetRef: useRef(false) — tracks whether current sheet was FAB-opened
- fabMorphSheet: useState(false) — mirror of ref for JSX reads (ESLint react-hooks/refs)
- handleFabMorphComplete(): sets fabMorphOpenedSheetRef=true, fabMorphSheet=true, opens sheet
- handleSheetClose(): if FAB-opened → startFabReverseRef.current() then setSheetOpen(false)
  (same render batch → no flicker); otherwise plain close
- AddEntrySheet receives noCloseAnimation={fabMorphSheet} → Sheet closeImmediately

── TABBAR ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Forwards onFabMorphComplete + startFabReverseRef through to FloatingTabBar.

── SHEET: closeImmediately PROP ─────────────────────────────────────────────
New optional prop closeImmediately?: boolean. When true, the × button calls
onClose() immediately instead of running the 300ms slide-down animation.
Prevents competing animations during reverse morph.

── SEGMENTEDCONTROL: optionClassName PROP ───────────────────────────────────
New optional prop optionClassName?: string. AddEntrySheet passes "w-[90px]"
for fixed-width 90px tabs.

── ADDENTRYSHEET PICKER UI FIXES ────────────────────────────────────────────
- Tab bar: SegmentedControl (Food/Activity/Weight, optionClassName="w-[90px]")
  in Sheet stickyHeader slot. Replaces old underline tab button row + tabCls()
  helper. noCloseAnimation prop plumbed through to Sheet closeImmediately.
- FoodPicker container: rounded-[24px] (was 16px).
- Recent list: .slice(0, 4) (was 5).
- Section headers ("Recent", "Other methods"): replaced <SectionLabel> with
  <p className="px-1 pt-3 pb-2 text-callout font-semibold text-content">.
  Sentence case, 16pt, text-content (was uppercase, content-secondary).
- Method cards: h-[72px] w-[82px], text-subhead text-content icons+labels.
  Container: py-2 -my-1 overflowY:visible — lets card shadows bleed outside
  the sunken box without clipping. Border removed from card.
- List item wrapper: rounded-[16px] (was 12px).
- Log it always visible: removed hasItems guard from useSheetSetFooter.
  logBasket(): early return with showToast?.('Add something first') when empty.
- SVG icons (fill="currentColor") replacing old stroke icons:
  CameraMethodIcon — corner brackets + camera lens from camera.svg
  PhotoMethodIcon  — image frame + mountain thumbnail from photo.svg
  DescribeMethodIcon — document + lines from describe.svg
  LabelMethodIcon  — document + camera overlay from label.svg
  ManualMethodIcon — document with T-text from manual.svg
- SectionLabel import removed (no longer used).
- hasItems and logLabel variables removed from FoodForm (no longer used).

── TYPECHECK / LINT ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
tsc: clean. lint: clean.
Issues fixed during session:
  · hasItems and logLabel unused after removing guard (removed variables).

  · openMenu unused in FloatingTabBar — block-commented, void _openMenu trick.

    state mirror to satisfy react-hooks/refs ESLint rule.

── DESIGN SPEC (leve-add-entry-design-spec.docx v1.1) ───────────────────────
15-section Word document, 733 paragraphs, 5.4 MB.
Sections:
  1.  Overview & Principles (basket-first, meal-centric, camera-primary)
  2.  Design Tokens (colours, radii, spacing, typography from index.css)
  3.  Add Sheet (props, header, CTA, tabs, motion specs, z-index stack)
  4.  Food Pane (layout, 5 embedded Figma screenshots, empty/item states)
  5.  Camera Overlay
  6.  Photo Overlay
  7.  Describe Overlay
  8.  Label Overlay (+ Serving Modal)
  9.  Manual Entry Overlay
  10. Image Hero / Collage
  11. Edit Item Overlay
  12. Analysis Overlay
  13. Component Inventory (maps to actual AddEntrySheet.tsx)
  14. Open Items & V2 Considerations
  15. Codebase Reference (spec-to-type mapping, kit components, infra)
  App. Figma frame hyperlinks + prototype reference

Key spec details:
- Data model: basket item = { item: FoodItem, qty: number, unit?: 'srv' }
  macros(item, qty, unit): scale = unit==='srv' ? qty : qty/100
- FoodItem.measurementType: 'per_100g' | 'per_serving'
- FoodItem.referenceAmount = 100 (per_100g) | serving size in g (per_serving)
- Basket default: qty=100, unit undefined (per_100g) or qty=1, unit='srv'
- mealData: { name: string; photo?: string; items: MealItem[] } on FoodEntry
- Motion: overlay slide 280ms cubic-bezier(0.4,0,0.2,1); analysis fade 160ms
- Z-index: panes=1, edit/manual=40, method overlays=42, analysis=50, modal=52
- Image hero: single rounded rect (1 photo); 3-layer card fan collage (2–3)

  only because that was an example state, not the default.
