ROUND 71 — WEEKLY WEIGHT CADENCE + KG/LBS UNIT CONVERSION (2026-06-17)
================================================================

── WEEKLY WEIGHT CADENCE ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
User.weightCadence ('daily'|'weekly') and User.weeklyWeightDay (0=Mon…6=Sun)
added to types.ts. Both optional; absence treated as 'daily'.

AccountScreen: new "Tracking" section with WeightCadenceCard.
  - Daily/Weekly SegmentedControl saves to repos.user immediately.
  - When weekly: Mon–Sun pill picker appears; tapping a day saves weeklyWeightDay.
  - Descriptive note below the picker reflects current selection.

ProfileSheet: Units segmented control (kg / lbs) added to the edit-profile sheet.
  Previously the ProfileRow showed "Units" as a read-only label with no way to
  change it. Now it's a SegmentedControl in the sheet; saves with other profile fields.

TodayScreen DayPanel: receives weightCadence, weeklyWeightDay, and units props.
  isWeighInDay flag gates the evening weight reminder:
    - daily cadence → reminder every evening
    - weekly cadence → reminder only on the chosen weekday
  DOW conversion: JS Date.getDay() (0=Sun) → 0=Mon index to match weeklyWeightDay.

── KG/LBS UNIT CONVERSION ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
The domain/units.ts helpers (kgToLbs, lbsToKg, displayWeight) already existed
but were unused. All weight display and input sites now respect user.units.

GoalScreen:
  - All fmtKg(x) + " kg" sites replaced with displayWeight(x, units).
  - KgWeekChart y-axis tick labels convert to lbs; integers shown when lbs
    (Math.round(kgToLbs(v))) to avoid fractional clutter.
  - KgWeekChart and GoalOutcomeView are sub-components without `user` in scope:
    added units?: 'kg'|'lbs' prop to each, passed from GoalScreen (user?.units ?? 'kg').
    Replaced all user?.units references in their bodies with the units prop.
  - WeekChart (separate function, has user prop) uses user?.units directly.

GoalSetupScreen:
  - userUnits loaded in useLive and passed to GoalSetupForm.
  - toDisplay(kg)/toKg(v) helpers inside form: wraps all field init,
    typeStash restore, and save path.
  - Input labels: "Start (kg)"/"Target (kg)" → dynamic template literals.
  - goalIntensity() always called with kg (toKg applied to sNum/tNum).
  - Intensity summary (kgToLose, kgPerWeek) shown in user units.

AddEntrySheet WeightForm:
  - Loads user.units via useLive.
  - Field label: "Weight (kg)" → "Weight (lbs)" when units='lbs'.
  - Prefill value: converts kg → lbs for display.
  - "Previously X kg" note: also converted.
  - Save: converts display value back to kg before upsertForDate.

AccountScreen goal card: fmtKg → displayWeight for "Active · lose X" line.
TodayScreen weight tile: fmtKg(dayWeightKg) → displayWeight(dayWeightKg, units).

── SCOPE DECISION ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Macro nutrients (protein, carbs, fat) confirmed to stay in grams. Ounces is
not a nutrition unit — US labels, all fitness apps, and dietary literature
universally use grams for macros even in imperial-unit countries.

ROUND 70 — CTA VALIDATION + KCAL LABEL + MACRO BARS + TRACKING REDESIGN + EDIT PLAN FLOW + SLIDER/SHEET/THUMB/SPLASH POLISH (2026-06-16)
================================================================

── "YOUR PLAN" CTA: ALWAYS ENABLED + INLINE FIELD ERRORS ────────────────────
GoalSetupScreen previously disabled the CTA until all fields were valid.
This created a confusing "why won't it let me tap?" experience.

New behaviour:
  - CTA never has a `disabled` prop.
  - On tap: validate all required fields; if any fail, set fieldErrors state
    and return early without saving.
  - Error display: LabeledInput receives `invalid` prop (red border); error
    message rendered as <p className="mt-1 text-footnote text-danger"> below.

fieldErrors shape: { start?: string; target?: string; date?: string; startDate?: string }

Weight direction validation:
  - Lose goal: if +target >= +start → error "Target must be less than current weight"
  - Gain goal: if +target <= +start → error "Target must be greater than current weight"

── "KCAL LEFT" → "KCAL AVAILABLE" ──────────────────────────────────────────
Two occurrences renamed:
  1. TodayScreen.tsx — lose goal branch in the gauge card label
  2. OnboardingDailyAllowance.tsx — same label in the onboarding allowance screen

"kcal available" more accurately reflects that remaining calories are available
to eat, not that they are "left over" (which implies excess).

── TODAYSCREEN GAUGE CARD: MACRO BARS REDESIGN ──────────────────────────────
The `ProteinBar` component was replaced with `MacroBarsRow` (new component built
inline) showing consumed amounts across protein, carbs, and fat.

Logic for which bars appear:
  - Protein: always shown for both lose and gain goals (with progress bar toward target).
  - Carbs + fat: shown for gain goals where macroStyle is set (i.e. detailed tracking).
    Hidden for simple tracking (macroStyle === null) and for lose goals (only protein shown).
  - Carbs bar has a target only for lower_carb macroStyle; fat bar has a target for
    balanced and performance macroStyle (not lower_carb).
  - All bar values show consumed amounts (not static targets).

Consumed carbs and fat computed in DayPanel:
  const carbs = day ? Math.round(day.foods.reduce((s, f) =>
    s + effectiveNutrition(f, day.itemsById).carbs, 0)) : 0;
  const fat = day ? Math.round(day.foods.reduce((s, f) =>
    s + effectiveNutrition(f, day.itemsById).fat, 0)) : 0;

DayPanelProps gains: macroStyle?: string, fatTargetG?: number, carbLimitG?: number.
DayPanel call passes: goal?.macroStyle, goal?.fatTargetG, goal?.carbLimitG.

AccountScreen macro visibility toggles (r68 feature) intentionally removed going
forward — macroStyle from goal setup now drives visibility instead.

── TRACKING STEP REDESIGN ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
Before (r65):
  - SegmentedControl with Simple / Detailed tabs.
  - Simple mode showed a simple summary.
  - Macro style cards only visible when Detailed tab was selected.

After (r70):
  - SegmentedControl removed entirely. Simple mode removed.
  - Info text shows free prose (text-callout, text-content-secondary, no icon/container).
  - All three macro style cards (Balanced, Performance, Lower-carb) start deselected.
  - Tapping a card toggles it on; tapping again deselects (back to no macro tracking).
  - Macro target inputs (protein/carbs/fat sliders) only rendered when a card is selected
    (macroStyle !== null). Protein row removed from tracking step (uses computed default).
  - "Skip" CTA saves with macroStyle = null → derived trackingMode = 'simple'.

State changes:
  - macroStyle: useState<MacroStyle | null>(activeGoal?.macroStyle ?? null)
    (was never-null before)
  - trackingMode: no longer stored in state; derived at save time:
    trackingMode = macroStyle ? 'detailed' : 'simple'
  - create() no longer accepts overrideMode parameter.
  - TrackingMode removed from imports in GoalSetupScreen.

── EDIT GOAL: SKIP TYPE SELECTION ───────────────────────────────────────────
Previously editing a goal required stepping through the type selection screen
before reaching "Your plan" details.

New behaviour:
  - Step init: (skipType || editing) ? 'details' : 'choose'
  - goBackFromDetails: closes the flow when skipType || editing (no back step).
  - Title: editing ? 'Edit plan' : 'Your plan'

This matches the intent of the "Edit plan" action in GoalSettingsSheet — the user
already has a goal type; they just want to adjust targets/dates/macros.

── SLIDER SPACING (MACROROW + DEFICIT SLIDER) ───────────────────────────────
Gap between labeled field and slider thumb, and between slider and note/label below:
  Before: mt-2 = 8px
  After:  mt-[2px] = 2px

Applied to MacroRow component and the deficit slider in GoalSetupScreen.
Reduces vertical sprawl in the tracking setup step.

── SHEET EXPANDED HEIGHT: 91dvh ─────────────────────────────────────────────
Sheet.tsx: expanded/forceExpanded height and maxHeight changed from 90dvh → 91dvh.
Recovers 1vh of content area on tall sheets (Add Entry, Goal Setup, etc.).

── SLIDER THUMB REACTIVITY ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Problem: Previous implementation animated scale-up on press. On iOS WKWebView,
the 200ms animation meant the thumb only started scaling after the user had
already begun dragging, making it feel unresponsive.

Fix (src/index.css):
  input[type="range"]::-webkit-slider-thumb {
    transition: transform 200ms cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);
  }
  input[type="range"]:active::-webkit-slider-thumb {
    transform: scale(2);
    transition: transform 0ms;       /* ← instant scale-up on press */
  }

Scale-up is now instant (0ms) so the thumb expands the moment finger lands.
Scale-down uses the 200ms spring so the release feels fluid.

── SPLASH SCREEN GAUGE ANIMATION: REVISED TIMINGS ───────────────────────────
LeveLoadingScreen.tsx: total animation cycle 1.5s → 1.2s.

Phase breakdown (1.2s = 1200ms):
  Pause    : 100ms  (8.3% of cycle)
  Grow     : 300ms  (33.3%)
  Slide    : 400ms  (66.7%)
  Collapse : 300ms  (91.7%)
  Pause    : 100ms  (100%)

CSS: `animation: leve-arc-fill 1.2s linear alternate infinite`
Keyframe percentages updated to match new phase boundaries.

tsc: clean.
